热消融对 40 岁以上丹麦女性持续性 HPV 感染的影响:一项半随机临床研究

Effect of thermal ablation on persistent HPV infection in Danish women ≥ 40 years of age: a quasi-randomized clinical study
作者： Trine Nielsen, Charlotte Floridon, Gitte Bennich, Birgitte Tønnes Pedersen, Helle Pedersen, Lene von Kappelgaard, Reza Serizawa, Estrid Høgdall, Jesper Bonde, Estrid Hogdall
摘要中文翻译热消融是一种宫颈热 治疗,可破坏宫颈的特定区域。我们使用 HPV 状态和 FAM19A4/miR124-2 DNA 甲基化作为 治疗 疗效的分子标记,评估热消融是否有助于持续高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染的女性的 HPV 清除。共有 131 名年龄≥ 40 岁、持续感染 HR-HPV 但没有组织学证实的高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变 (≥ CIN2) 的女性在三个妇科诊所入组。排除后,39 名女性被分配到干预组(热消融),34 名女性被分配到仅无 治疗 的对照组。使用准随机化,根据出生日期进行分配(偶数日期:干预;奇数日期:对照)。在基线(0 个月)、3 个月和 10 个月时评估 HPV 基因分型和 FAM19A4/miR124-2 甲基化状态。使用 WISAP 探针在 65°C 下进行热消融 2 分钟,在 100°C 下进行 40 秒,分两次进行,间隔 2-3 天。10 个月时,干预组中 64.1% 的女性和对照组中 79.4% 的女性仍保持 HR-HPV 阳性。在任何时间点均未观察到各组间 HPV 阳性率存在显着差异。FAM19A4/miR124-2 甲基化阳性率在 3 个月时在干预组中显示短暂下降 (-10.2%),但在 10 个月后两组中均增加至基线水平以上,但没有显着的 治疗 效应。对于持续性 HR-HPV 感染且无明显宫颈疾病的女性,热消融不能促进 HPV 清除。两个研究组的甲基化阳性率随着时间的推移而增加,这与正在进行的致癌转化一致。接受热消融治疗的女性仍保持 HR-HPV 阳性,需要继续 监测,这表明与单独观察相比没有临床益处。ClinicalTrials.gov,NCT07376031,注册于 2026 年 1 月 21 日。热消融不会显着影响 ≥40 岁持续 高危型HPV 感染且无明显宫颈疾病的女性的 HPV 清除或 FAM19A4/miR124-2 甲基化状态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管没有高级别病变,但年龄≥40岁的女性持续高危人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV)感染仍存在进展为宫颈疾病的风险。该研究旨在利用 HPV 状态和 FAM19A4/miR124-2 DNA 甲基化作为 治疗 功效的分子标记,确定热消融是否有助于该人群的 HPV 清除。
设计与对象
一项半随机临床研究在三个妇科门诊招募了 131 名年龄≥40 岁、持续 HR-HPV 感染但无组织学证实的高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变 (≥CIN2) 的女性。排除后,39 名女性被分配到热消融组,34 名女性被分配到非 治疗 对照组,使用基于出生日期的准随机化。
核心方法
在基线、3 个月和 10 个月时评估 HPV 基因分型和 FAM19A4/miR124-2 甲基化状态。使用 WISAP 探头在 65 °C 下进行热消融 2 分钟,在 100 °C 下进行 40 秒,分两次进行,间隔 2-3 天。
主要结果
10 个月时,干预组中 64.1% 的女性和对照组中 79.4% 的女性仍保持 HR-HPV 阳性,两组之间在任何时间点都没有显着差异。FAM19A4/miR124-2 甲基化阳性率在 3 个月时在干预组中显示短暂下降 (-10.2%),但在 10 个月后两组中均增加至基线水平以上,但没有显着的 治疗 效应。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,对于持续性 HR-HPV 感染但无明显宫颈疾病的女性,热消融不会促进 HPV 清除或降低致癌转化风险,正如双臂持续 HPV 阳性和甲基化阳性增加所证明的那样。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对于该人群中的 HPV 清除,热消融并没有比单独观察提供临床益处,这表明持续的 监测 仍然是必要的,并且应探索针对 ≥ 40 岁女性持续性 HR-HPV 感染的替代策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的准随机设计和缺乏对高级病变的组织学确认限制了因果推断和普遍性。由于样本量小、未实施盲法以及依赖分子标志物而不是临床终点,证据强度中等。

Effect of thermal ablation on persistent HPV infection in Danish women ≥ 40 years of age: a quasi-randomized clinical study

Authors: Trine Nielsen, Charlotte Floridon, Gitte Bennich, Birgitte Tønnes Pedersen, Helle Pedersen, Lene von Kappelgaard, Reza Serizawa, Estrid Høgdall, Jesper Bonde, Estrid Hogdall
Original abstract (English)
Thermal ablation is a cervical heat treatment that destroys a defined area of the cervix. We evaluated whether thermal ablation facilitates HPV clearance in women with persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection, using HPV status and FAM19A4/miR124-2 DNA methylation as molecular markers for treatment efficacy. A total of 131 women aged ≥ 40 years with persistent HR-HPV infection but without histologically confirmed high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (≥ CIN2) were enrolled at three outpatient gynecological clinics. After exclusions, 39 women were allocated to the intervention arm (thermal ablation) and 34 to the no treatment only control arm. Allocation was based on date of birth (even dates: intervention; odd dates: control), using quasi-randomization. HPV genotyping and FAM19A4/miR124-2 methylation status were assessed at baseline (0 months), 3 months, and 10 months. Thermal ablation was performed using the WISAP probe at 65 °C for 2 min and at 100 °C for 40 s, administered across two sessions 2–3 days apart. At 10 months, 64.1% of women in the intervention arm and 79.4% in the control arm remained HR-HPV positive. No significant difference in HPV positivity was observed between arms at any time point. FAM19A4/miR124-2 methylation positivity showed a transient decrease in the intervention arm (-10.2%) at 3 months but increased in both arms over 10 months to above baseline levels, with no significant treatment effect. Thermal ablation does not facilitate HPV clearance in women with persistent HR-HPV infection without significant cervical disease. Methylation positivity increased over time in both study arms, consistent with ongoing oncogenic transformation. Women treated with thermal ablation remain HR-HPV positive and require continued surveillance, indicating no clinical benefit over observation alone. ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT07376031, registered 21 January 2026. Thermal ablation does not significantly affect HPV clearance or FAM19A4/miR124-2 methylation status in women ≥40 years with persistent high-risk HPV infection without significant cervical disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection in women aged ≥40 years poses a risk for progression to cervical disease despite the absence of high-grade lesions. The study aimed to determine whether thermal ablation facilitates HPV clearance in this population using HPV status and FAM19A4/miR124-2 DNA methylation as molecular markers for treatment efficacy.
Design and population
A quasi-randomized clinical study enrolled 131 women aged ≥40 years with persistent HR-HPV infection but without histologically confirmed high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (≥CIN2) across three outpatient gynecological clinics. After exclusions, 39 women were allocated to thermal ablation and 34 to a no-treatment control arm using quasi-randomization based on date of birth.
Core methods
HPV genotyping and FAM19A4/miR124-2 methylation status were assessed at baseline, 3 months, and 10 months. Thermal ablation was performed using the WISAP probe at 65 °C for 2 minutes and at 100 °C for 40 seconds, administered across two sessions 2–3 days apart.
Main results
At 10 months, 64.1% of women in the intervention arm and 79.4% in the control arm remained HR-HPV positive, with no significant difference between arms at any time point. FAM19A4/miR124-2 methylation positivity showed a transient decrease (-10.2%) in the intervention arm at 3 months but increased in both arms over 10 months to above baseline levels, with no significant treatment effect.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that thermal ablation does not facilitate HPV clearance or reduce oncogenic transformation risk in women with persistent HR-HPV infection without significant cervical disease, as evidenced by sustained HPV positivity and increasing methylation positivity in both arms.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that thermal ablation does not provide clinical benefit over observation alone for HPV clearance in this population, indicating that continued surveillance remains necessary and that alternative strategies should be explored for persistent HR-HPV infection in women ≥40 years.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s quasi-randomized design and lack of histological confirmation of high-grade lesions limit causal inference and generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to small sample size, absence of blinding, and reliance on molecular markers rather than clinical endpoints.

Journal: American journal of public health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-20  |  DOI: 10.2105/ajph.2026.308635  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e1-e4
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

入学 疫苗接种 规定对青少年 疫苗接种 疫苗接种 吸收率的影响:美国,2008-2023 年。

Effects of School-Entry Vaccination Mandates on Adolescent Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Uptake: United States, 2008-2023.
作者： Angela W Chen, Edwin S Wong, Rachel L Winer, Monisha Sharma, Kristen E Gray
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了为什么尽管有国家建议,美国青少年的 HPV 疫苗接种 感染率仍然不理想,并发现在理解州级入学 疫苗接种 要求对提高覆盖率的影响方面存在差距。
设计与对象
一项准实验性双重差异研究使用 2008-2023 年国家免疫调查青少年 13 至 17 岁青少年的经提供者验证的记录,比较了 4 个强制州和 47 个非强制州和地区的 HPV 疫苗接种 吸收(≥1 剂)。
核心方法
该分析考虑了交错的政策实施,并使用来自国家免疫调查青少年的提供者验证的 疫苗接种 记录对人口和社会经济协变量进行了调整。
主要结果
School-entry mandates were associated with 6.4 percentage points (95% CI = 3.6, 9.1) higher HPV vaccination uptake among girls and 3.4 percentage points (95% CI = 1.8, 5.0) higher uptake among boys.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明强制规定与较高的 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取率相关,并指出,随着时间的推移,这种关联在女孩中会增强,但在最初实施后,在男孩中会减弱。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,入学强制措施可能是州级战略的重要组成部分,以增加 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收并减少 HPV 相关癌症,但仅靠强制措施不太可能实现国家目标。
局限与证据强度
由于准实验设计、潜在的不可测量的混杂因素以及对提供者验证记录的依赖,证据的强度中等,限制了所包括的州和地区之外的因果推断和普遍性。

Effects of School-Entry Vaccination Mandates on Adolescent Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Uptake: United States, 2008-2023.

Authors: Angela W Chen, Edwin S Wong, Rachel L Winer, Monisha Sharma, Kristen E Gray
Original abstract (English)
To evaluate the association between school-entry vaccination mandates and adolescent human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination uptake in the United States. We conducted a quasi-experimental study using a difference-in-differences approach comparing HPV vaccination uptake (≥ 1 dose) in mandate (n = 4) versus nonmandate (n = 47) states and territories by sex. We used provider-verified vaccination records from the 2008-2023 National Immunization Survey-Teen (n = 240 616) for adolescents aged 13 to 17 years. Models accounted for staggered policy implementation and adjusted for demographic and socioeconomic covariates. School-entry mandates were associated with 6.4 percentage-points (95% confidence interval [CI] = 3.6, 9.1) higher HPV vaccination uptake among girls and 3.4 percentage points (95% CI = 1.8, 5.0) higher uptake among boys. Associations with HPV vaccination uptake increased over time for girls, whereas for boys, associations were concentrated in the first few years following implementation and attenuated thereafter. School-entry mandates are associated with higher HPV vaccination uptake among adolescents. Public Health Implications. Although mandates alone are unlikely to achieve national HPV vaccination targets, they may serve as an important component of comprehensive state efforts to increase uptake and prevent HPV-related cancers. (Am J Public Health. Published online ahead of print August 20, 2026:e1-e4. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308635).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why HPV vaccination uptake among adolescents in the United States remained suboptimal despite national recommendations, identifying a gap in understanding the impact of state-level school-entry vaccination mandates on improving coverage.
Design and population
A quasi-experimental difference-in-differences study compared HPV vaccination uptake (≥1 dose) between four mandate states and 47 nonmandate states and territories using provider-verified records from the 2008–2023 National Immunization Survey-Teen for adolescents aged 13 to 17 years.
Core methods
The analysis accounted for staggered policy implementation and adjusted for demographic and socioeconomic covariates using provider-verified vaccination records from the National Immunization Survey-Teen.
Main results
School-entry mandates were associated with 6.4 percentage points (95% CI = 3.6, 9.1) higher HPV vaccination uptake among girls and 3.4 percentage points (95% CI = 1.8, 5.0) higher uptake among boys.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that mandates are associated with higher HPV vaccination uptake, noting that associations strengthened over time for girls but attenuated for boys after initial implementation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests school-entry mandates may serve as an important component of state-level strategies to increase HPV vaccination uptake and reduce HPV-related cancers, though mandates alone are unlikely to achieve national targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is moderate in strength due to the quasi-experimental design, potential unmeasured confounding, and reliance on provider-verified records, limiting causal inference and generalizability beyond the included states and territories.

HPV16 基因组状态对口咽鳞状细胞癌疾病复发的风险进行分层

HPV16 Genomic Status Stratifies Risk of Disease Recurrence in Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma
作者： Victor Malassigné, Julien Puech, Sandrine Imbeaud, Anne Auperin, Caroline Even, Dac Hung Nguyen, Maxime Wack, Bastien Rance, Léa Benyakar, Léa Picavet
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的:尽管预后总体良好,但 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 患者的一部分仍会出现疾病复发。仍然缺乏用于风险分层的可靠生物标志物。我们研究了全面的病毒基因组分析是否可以改善预后分类。实验设计:使用靶向病毒捕获测序对参加 2 个独立 队列研究 的 100 名 p16 阳性和 HPV DNA 阳性 OPSCC 患者的肿瘤样本进行分析。对病毒基因型、亚系、单核苷酸变异、病毒-宿主整合位点、病毒结构变异(SV)、克隆性和病毒拷贝数进行了表征。在 60 个月时评估与累积 发病率 进展、无进展生存期 (PFS) 和总生存期的关联。结果:94个肿瘤中检测到HPV16,其中83个属于HPV16_A1亚系。在该亚系中,20 个肿瘤具有严格附加型病毒 基因组,而 63 个则表现出整合和/或病毒 SV。在严格附加型 HPV16_A1 亚组中没有发生进展事件,而在具有整合和/或 SV 的 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中发生了 12 起进展事件。60 个月时,在严格附加型 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中没有观察到进展事件,而在具有整合和/或 SV 的 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中,累积进展 发病率 为 23.1%。先前报道的高风险单核苷酸变异和新型上游调控区变异与较差的 PFS 相关。整合位点在开放染色质和启动子区域富集,并聚集在病毒 E2 和 E4 基因内。结论:在 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中,病毒基因组结构定义了临床上不同的亚组。严格附加型(野生型)基因组 识别低风险人群,而整合和 SV 则表征高风险疾病。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管预后良好,但仍有一部分 HPV 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 患者出现疾病复发,并且仍然缺乏可靠的风险分层生物标志物。
设计与对象
使用靶向病毒捕获测序对参加 2 个独立 队列研究 的 100 名 p16 阳性和 HPV DNA 阳性 OPSCC 患者的肿瘤样本进行分析。
核心方法
对病毒基因型、亚系、单核苷酸变异、病毒-宿主整合位点、病毒结构变异、克隆性和病毒拷贝数进行了表征,并在60个月时评估了与累积发病率进展、无进展生存和总生存的关联。
主要结果
在94个肿瘤中检测到HPV16,其中83个属于HPV16_A1亚系;在该亚系中,20 个肿瘤具有严格附加型 基因组,63 个肿瘤表现出整合和/或病毒结构变异,并且在 60 个月时,严格附加型亚组中没有发生进展事件,而整合/SV 亚组中的累积 发病率 为 23.1%。
解释与创新
在 HPV16_A1 肿瘤中,病毒基因组结构定义了临床上不同的亚组,严格附加型 基因组 识别低风险群体,整合/SV 表征高风险疾病。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,病毒基因组分析可以改善 HPV16_A1 阳性 OPSCC 的预后分类,可能指导 监测 强度和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、针对 HPV16_A1 的单病毒关注以及对靶向测序的依赖而受到限制,需要前瞻性验证和更广泛的基因组分析来确认普遍性。

HPV16 Genomic Status Stratifies Risk of Disease Recurrence in Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma

Authors: Victor Malassigné, Julien Puech, Sandrine Imbeaud, Anne Auperin, Caroline Even, Dac Hung Nguyen, Maxime Wack, Bastien Rance, Léa Benyakar, Léa Picavet
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose: A subset of patients with human papillomavirus (HPV)–positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) develops disease recurrence despite generally favorable outcomes. Reliable biomarkers for risk stratification remain lacking. We investigated whether comprehensive viral genomic profiling refines prognostic classification. Experimental Design: Tumor samples from 100 patients with p16-positive and HPV DNA–positive OPSCC enrolled in 2 independent cohorts were analyzed using targeted viral capture sequencing. Viral genotype, sublineage, single-nucleotide variation, virus–host integration sites, viral structural variants (SV), clonality, and viral copy number were characterized. Associations with cumulative incidence of progression, progression-free survival (PFS), and overall survival were assessed at 60 months. Results: HPV16 was detected in 94 tumors, 83 of which belonged to the HPV16_A1 sublineage. Within this sublineage, 20 tumors harbored strictly episomal viral genomes, whereas 63 exhibited integration and/or viral SV. No progression events occurred in the strictly episomal HPV16_A1 subgroup, whereas 12 occurred among HPV16_A1 tumors with an integration and/or SV. At 60 months, no progression events were observed in strictly episomal HPV16_A1 tumors compared with a 23.1% cumulative incidence of progression in HPV16_A1 tumors with an integration and/or SV. Previously reported high-risk single-nucleotide variants and a novel upstream regulatory region variant were associated with worse PFS. Integration sites were enriched in open chromatin and promoter regions and clustered within viral E2 and E4 genes. Conclusions: Within HPV16_A1 tumors, viral genomic architecture defines clinically distinct subgroups. Strictly episomal (wild-type) genomes identify a low-risk population, whereas integration and SV characterize higher-risk disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A subset of patients with HPV-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) experiences disease recurrence despite generally favorable outcomes, and reliable biomarkers for risk stratification remain lacking.
Design and population
Tumor samples from 100 patients with p16-positive and HPV DNA–positive OPSCC enrolled in 2 independent cohorts were analyzed using targeted viral capture sequencing.
Core methods
Viral genotype, sublineage, single-nucleotide variation, virus–host integration sites, viral structural variants, clonality, and viral copy number were characterized, and associations with cumulative incidence of progression, progression-free survival, and overall survival were assessed at 60 months.
Main results
HPV16 was detected in 94 tumors, with 83 belonging to the HPV16_A1 sublineage; within this sublineage, 20 tumors had strictly episomal genomes and 63 exhibited integration and/or viral structural variants, and no progression events occurred in the strictly episomal subgroup compared with a 23.1% cumulative incidence in the integration/SV subgroup at 60 months.
Interpretation and novelty
Within HPV16_A1 tumors, viral genomic architecture defines clinically distinct subgroups, with strictly episomal genomes identifying a low-risk population and integration/SV characterizing higher-risk disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that viral genomic profiling could refine prognostic classification in HPV16_A1–positive OPSCC, potentially guiding surveillance intensity and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, single-virus focus on HPV16_A1, and reliance on targeted sequencing, warranting prospective validation and broader genomic analyses to confirm generalizability.

人乳头瘤病毒 16型感染且宫颈上皮内瘤变≤1级患者PVX2免疫治疗后病毒清除的免疫相关性探讨

Exploration of immune correlates of viral clearance after PVX2 immunotherapy in patients with human papillomavirus type 16 infection and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1
作者： Vikrant Palande, Tianbei Zhang, Kellie N. Smith, Kimberly Levinson, Leslie Cope, Hua-Ling Tsai, Yung-Nien Chang, T.-C. Wu, Mark H. Einstein, Richard B. S. Roden
摘要中文翻译摘要 在 PVX2 免疫治疗治疗的 CIN ≤ 1 例患者中寻找与 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV16) 清除率相关的 免疫应答。PVX2 包含肌肉注射 疫苗接种,其中含有 HPV16 E7 靶向 pNGVL4a-Sig/E7(detox)/HSP70 DNA 疫苗(3 mg,第 0 周和第 4 周)和 HPV16 L2E7E6 融合蛋白(TA-CIN;100 μg 第 8 周)。对 12 名 HPV16+ CIN ≤1 的女性施用 PVX2 引发了抗体和 CD8+ T 细胞反应,包括 TCR 多样性的变化、克隆扩张和公共克隆型的出现。第 6 个月时,5/11 名可评估参与者中未检测到 HPV16。疫苗接种 后 E6、E7 和 L2 特异性抗体滴度较高,但与清除率无关。TCR 克隆多样性/扩增和公共克隆型均与 HPV16 清除无关。虽然扩展/新克隆型的分数与 HPV16 清除无关,但在那些清除 HPV16 的人中,疫苗 抗原特异性读数在 疫苗接种 后的比例明显较高。高基线血清 MCP-1 与 HPV16 持续存在相关,而在清除 HPV16 的患者中,疫苗接种 后 MIP-1β 呈较高趋势。重要性 HPV16 是超过一半的 宫颈癌 的必要原因,在其他肛门生殖器和口咽癌中的比例甚至更高。考虑到当前 治疗 的疾病负担和局限性,需要针对 HPV16 感染和相关癌前病变的 治疗。持续感染 HPV 的女性由于频繁进行阴道镜检查、不便、主动 监测 带来的医疗费用以及将病毒传播给其他性伴侣的可能性而承受着焦虑和压力。由于大多数患者可以自发清除感染,因此免疫治疗可以促进消除疾病可能进展的持续性感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV16) 感染是宫颈癌和其他肛门生殖器癌症的必要原因,但许多感染会自行清除,因此需要免疫疗法来安全促进持续病例中的病毒清除。该研究旨在确定 HPV16 阳性宫颈上皮内瘤变 ≤1 级女性在 PVX2 免疫治疗后与 HPV16 清除相关的免疫相关性。
设计与对象
在临床环境中,对 12 名患有 HPV16 阳性宫颈上皮内瘤变 ≤ 1 级的女性进行了 PVX2 免疫治疗,并随着时间的推移对 免疫应答 进行评估,以评估病毒清除的相关性。
核心方法
PVX2 由肌内注射 HPV16 E7 靶向 pNGVL4a-Sig/E7(排毒)/HSP70 DNA 疫苗(第 0 周和第 4 周 3 mg)组成,随后是 HPV16 L2E7E6 融合蛋白(TA-CIN;第 8 周 100 μg),疫苗接种 后免疫分析包括抗体滴度、CD8+ T 细胞反应、TCR 多样性和细胞因子测量。
主要结果
第 6 个月时,11 名可评估参与者中有 5 名未检测到 HPV16,而 疫苗接种 后 E6、E7 和 L2 特异性抗体滴度较高,但与清除率不相关。TCR 克隆多样性/扩展和公共克隆型均与 HPV16 清除无关,但在清除 HPV16 的患者中,在 疫苗 后观察到较高比例的 疫苗 抗原特异性读数。
解释与创新
作者解释说,仅抗体滴度和 TCR 库变化并不能预测 HPV16 清除率,这表明 疫苗 抗原特异性 免疫应答 可能比全局 TCR 指标更相关。他们强调 疫苗 抗原特异性读数的出现是一种潜在的新关联,值得进一步研究。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现谨慎地表明,PVX2 免疫疗法可能会促进一些患有低度病变的女性的 HPV16 清除,从而可能减少对侵入性 监测 的需求以及相关的医疗负担,尽管保护的相关性尚未完全确定。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小(n = 12)且缺乏对照组限制了因果推断和普遍性,并且对仅抽象证据的依赖进一步限制了有关清除率免疫相关性的结论的强度。

Exploration of immune correlates of viral clearance after PVX2 immunotherapy in patients with human papillomavirus type 16 infection and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1

Authors: Vikrant Palande, Tianbei Zhang, Kellie N. Smith, Kimberly Levinson, Leslie Cope, Hua-Ling Tsai, Yung-Nien Chang, T.-C. Wu, Mark H. Einstein, Richard B. S. Roden
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Immune responses correlated with clearance of human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16) were sought in PVX2 immunotherapy-treated CIN ≤1 patients. PVX2 comprises intramuscular vaccinations with HPV16 E7-targeted pNGVL4a-Sig/E7(detox)/HSP70 DNA vaccine (3 mg, weeks 0 and 4) and HPV16 L2E7E6 fusion protein (TA-CIN; 100 μg week 8). Administering PVX2 to 12 women with HPV16+ CIN ≤1 elicited both antibody and CD8+ T-cell responses, including changes in TCR diversity, clonal expansion, and emergence of public clonotypes. HPV16 was not detected in 5/11 evaluable participants at month 6. Post-vaccination E6, E7, and L2-specific antibody titers were higher, but none correlated with clearance. Neither TCR clonal diversity/expansion nor public clonotypes were associated with HPV16 clearance. While the fractions of expanded/new clonotypes were not associated with HPV16 clearance, a higher proportion of vaccine antigen-specific reads was evident post-vaccination in those clearing HPV16. High baseline serum MCP-1 associated with HPV16 persistence, and MIP-1β trended higher post-vaccination in those clearing HPV16. IMPORTANCE HPV16 is a necessary cause of over half of all cervical cancers, and in even higher fractions of other anogenital and oropharyngeal cancers. Treatments for HPV16 infection and associated pre-cancerous lesions are needed given the disease burden and limitations of current treatments. Women with persistent HPV infections endure anxiety and stress due to frequent colposcopies, inconvenience, healthcare costs from active surveillance, and potential to transmit the virus to other sexual partners. Since a majority of patients can spontaneously clear their infections, administration of an immunotherapy may promote the elimination of otherwise persistent infections where disease can progress.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16) infection is a necessary cause of cervical and other anogenital cancers, yet many infections clear spontaneously, creating a need for immunotherapies that safely promote viral clearance in persistent cases. The study aimed to identify immune correlates associated with HPV16 clearance following PVX2 immunotherapy in women with HPV16-positive cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1.
Design and population
PVX2 immunotherapy was administered to 12 women with HPV16-positive cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1 in a clinical setting, with immune responses assessed over time to evaluate correlates of viral clearance.
Core methods
PVX2 consisted of intramuscular HPV16 E7-targeted pNGVL4a-Sig/E7(detox)/HSP70 DNA vaccine (3 mg at weeks 0 and 4) followed by HPV16 L2E7E6 fusion protein (TA-CIN; 100 μg at week 8), with post-vaccination immune profiling including antibody titers, CD8+ T-cell responses, TCR diversity, and cytokine measurements.
Main results
HPV16 was not detected in 5 of 11 evaluable participants at month 6, while post-vaccination E6, E7, and L2-specific antibody titers were higher but did not correlate with clearance. Neither TCR clonal diversity/expansion nor public clonotypes were associated with HPV16 clearance, though a higher proportion of vaccine antigen-specific reads was observed post-vaccination in those clearing HPV16.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that antibody titers and TCR repertoire changes alone did not predict HPV16 clearance, suggesting that vaccine antigen-specific immune responses may be more relevant than global TCR metrics. They highlighted the emergence of vaccine antigen-specific reads as a potential novel correlate warranting further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings cautiously suggest that PVX2 immunotherapy may promote HPV16 clearance in some women with low-grade lesions, potentially reducing the need for invasive surveillance and associated healthcare burdens, though correlates of protection remain incompletely defined.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size (n=12) and lack of a control group limit causal inference and generalizability, and reliance on abstract-only evidence further constrains the strength of conclusions regarding immune correlates of clearance.

智利中北部地区公共卫生系统中的患病率 人乳头瘤病毒

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译目标 此前,我们发现在私人医疗系统就诊的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的 患病率 较高。因此,本研究的目的是确定在公共卫生系统就诊的大量 队列研究 女性中 HR-HPV 的频率,并比较这些结果。方法 使用 qPCR 和巴氏技术对 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本进行分析。结果 2020-2024年HR-HPV感染总体患病率为13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),2020年、2021年分别为13.07%、12.64%、14.80%、14.24%和14.15%。分别为 2022 年、2023 年和 2024 年。在25岁以下女性中,评估期间HR-HPV的患病率为34.03%,而25至44岁女性为16.35%,45至64岁女性为10.47%,64岁以上女性为11.52%。子宫颈抹片检查显示,HPV 阴性女性的 NILM 结果为 89.71%,而 HPV 阳性女性的 NILM 结果为 74.59%。HPV阴性女性中LSIL和HSIL的发生率分别为0.14%和0.07%;然而,在 HPV 阳性女性中,这些值分别增加到 4.73% 和 4.43%。HPV 阴性女性中只有 0.006% 可能患有癌症,而 HPV 阳性女性中则有 0.46% 可能患有癌症。结论 中北部地区公共卫生系统女性 HR-HPV 感染 患病率 似乎与智利之前报告的相似,但低于同一地区私营医疗系统女性报告的 患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要进行这项研究来确定就读公共卫生系统的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的频率,因为之前的研究发现私人卫生系统中的 患病率 较高。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究分析了智利中北部地区 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 和 Papanicolaou 技术对样本进行分析,以检测 HR-HPV 感染和细胞学异常。
主要结果
HR-HPV 感染的总体 患病率 为 13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),与年龄较大的群体相比,25 岁​​以下女性的感染率更高(34.03%)。HPV 阳性女性的 LSIL(4.73% vs. 0.14%)和 HSIL(4.43% vs. 0.07%)发生率显着高于 HPV 阴性女性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统中的 HR-HPV 患病率 与之前报告的全国比率相似,但低于同一地区的私人卫生系统,突显了医疗系统之间 HPV 感染率的潜在差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过提供按年龄分层的 监测 数据为公共卫生 监测 和资源分配提供信息,这可以指导公共卫生系统中 HPV 相关疾病的有针对性的筛查和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其大样本量和纵向设计,但其局限性包括潜在的选择偏差,因为仅包括参加妇科检查的女性,并且由于报告仅具有摘要性质,证据并不充分。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE Previously, we found a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the private health system. Therefore, the objective of this study was to determine the frequency of HR-HPV in a large cohort of women attending the public health system and to compare these findings. METHODS A total of 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique. RESULTS The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection between 2020 and 2024 was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with annual values of 13.07%, 12.64%, 14.80%, 14.24%, and 14.15% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. In women under 25 years of age, the prevalence of HR-HPV was 34.03% during the evaluated period, compared to 16.35% in women aged 25 to 44 years, 10.47% in those aged 45 to 64 years, and 11.52% in women over 64 years of age. Pap smears showed 89.71% NILM results in HPV-negative women, compared to 74.59% in HPV-positive women. The rates of LSIL and HSIL were 0.14% and 0.07% in HPV-negative women, respectively; however, these values increased to 4.73% and 4.43% in HPV-positive women, respectively. Probable carcinoma was found in only 0.006% of HPV-negative women, but in 0.46% of HPV-positive women. CONCLUSIONS The prevalence of HR-HPV infection in women attending the public health system in the central-northern region appears to be similar to that previously reported in Chile, but lower than that reported in the same region for women attending the private health system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to determine the frequency of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the public health system, as previous research had found a high prevalence in the private health system.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study analyzed 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 in the central-northern region of Chile.
Core methods
Samples were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique to detect HR-HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with higher rates in women under 25 years (34.03%) compared to older age groups. HPV-positive women had significantly higher rates of LSIL (4.73% vs. 0.14%) and HSIL (4.43% vs. 0.07%) than HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HR-HPV prevalence in the public health system was similar to previously reported national rates but lower than in the private health system in the same region, highlighting potential disparities in HPV infection rates between healthcare systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform public health surveillance and resource allocation by providing age-stratified prevalence data, which can guide targeted screening and prevention strategies for HPV-related diseases in the public health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its large sample size and longitudinal design, but limitations include potential selection bias as only women attending gynecological check-ups were included, and the evidence is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the report.

免疫信息学驱动的改变肽配体的合理设计以及针对 HPV16 E6/E7 的多表位 CTL 制剂的体外验证

Immunoinformatics-Driven Rational Design of Altered Peptide Ligands and In Vitro Validation of a Multi-Epitope CTL Formulation Targeting HPV16 E6/E7
作者： Dian Dong, Xiuqing Zhang, Bo Li
摘要中文翻译背景:针对 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV16) 的治疗 疫苗接种 经常受到天然 E6/E7 癌蛋白表位较弱的 HLA-A*02:01 呈现的限制。本研究旨在利用集成的计算实验流程来设计和评估锚定优化的改变肽配体 (APL),以改善肽呈递和 HPV16 特异性 T 细胞反应。方法:将锚残基取代引入三个野生型E6/E7表位。根据预测的呈现、亲和力、免疫原性和毒性,在计算机中对候选者进行优先排序,随后使用 HLA-A*02:01 阳性供体细胞和分子动力学 (MD) 模拟通过体外功能测定进行评估。结果:Anchor优化成功将弱Binder转化为强Binder;例如,E6apl 将预测亲和力从 329.33 nM 提高到 6.21 nM。至关重要的是,候选 E7apl1 表现出正常的 CD8+ T 细胞扩增,但减少了 IFN-γ 分泌,揭示了独特的结合-免疫原性解离。MD 模拟表明,改变的肽构象动力学可能导致功能活性的差异。随后,组装了优化的六肽制剂(三个 APL 和三个野生型表位)。由此产生的多表位 HPV 特异性细胞毒性 T 淋巴细胞 (meHPV-CTL) 介导针对 宫颈癌 细胞的靶标特异性细胞毒性,在效应器与靶标比率为 30:1 时实现 74.1% ± 5.1% 的特异性裂解,这在很大程度上被 HLA I 类阻断所消除。结论:这些概念验证结果表明,稳定的肽-MHC 结合是最佳 T 细胞激活的必要但不充分的条件。经过实验表征的多表位制剂为 HPV16 靶向肽免疫疗法的进一步临床前评估提供了概念验证策略。此外,由于这些锚定优化的 APL 是在序列水平上定义的,因此在未来的研究中,这些序列定义的 APL 可能会在替代的 疫苗 递送平台(包括基于 mRNA 的方法)中进行探索。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
针对 HPV16 的治疗性 疫苗接种 受到 HLA-A*02:01 天然 E6/E7 表位表达不良的限制,因此需要改进的免疫原。该研究试图设计锚定优化的改变肽配体 (APL),以增强肽呈递和 HPV16 特异性 T 细胞反应。
设计与对象
该研究使用集成的计算实验流程来设计和评估针对 HPV16 E6/E7 表位的 APL。将锚残基取代引入到三个野生型表位中,并在使用 HLA-A*02:01 阳性供体细胞进行体外功能测定和分子动力学模拟之前通过计算机进行优先排序。
核心方法
将锚残基取代引入到三个野生型 E6/E7 表位中,并根据预测的呈现、亲和力、免疫原性和毒性在计算机中对候选表位进行优先级排序。使用 HLA-A*02:01 阳性供体细胞和分子动力学模拟通过体外功能测定评估优先候选者。
主要结果
锚定优化将弱结合物转化为强结合物,E6apl 将预测亲和力从 329.33 nM 提高至 6.21 nM。候选 E7apl1 显示正常的 CD8+ T 细胞扩增,但 IFN-γ 分泌减少,表明结合免疫原性解离。六肽制剂以 30:1 的效应物与靶标比率介导 宫颈癌 细胞的 74.1% ± 5.1% 特异性裂解,很大程度上被 HLA I 类阻断所消除。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,稳定的肽-MHC 结合是必要的,但对于最佳 T 细胞激活来说还不够,这凸显了结合与功能活性之间的新分离。经过实验表征的多表位制剂为 HPV16 靶向肽免疫疗法提供了概念验证策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持 APL 的合理设计,以增强 HPV16 特异性 CTL 反应,可能为下一代基于肽的 疫苗 或免疫疗法提供信息。序列定义的 APL 可能适用于替代递送平台,包括基于 mRNA 的方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外概念验证,没有体内功效或安全性数据,降低了普遍性。由于缺乏临床终点并且依赖计算预测和离体测定,证据强度中等。

Immunoinformatics-Driven Rational Design of Altered Peptide Ligands and In Vitro Validation of a Multi-Epitope CTL Formulation Targeting HPV16 E6/E7

Authors: Dian Dong, Xiuqing Zhang, Bo Li
Original abstract (English)
Background: Therapeutic vaccination against human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16) is frequently limited by the weak HLA-A*02:01 presentation of native E6/E7 oncoprotein epitopes. This study aims to utilize an integrated computational-experimental pipeline to design and evaluate anchor-optimized altered peptide ligands (APLs) for improving peptide presentation and HPV16-specific T-cell responses. Methods: Anchor-residue substitutions were introduced into three wild-type E6/E7 epitopes. Candidates were prioritized in silico based on predicted presentation, affinity, immunogenicity, and toxicity, and subsequently evaluated via in vitro functional assays using HLA-A*02:01-positive donor cells and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. Results: Anchor optimization successfully converted weak binders into strong binders; for instance, E6apl improved predicted affinity from 329.33 to 6.21 nM. Crucially, candidate E7apl1 exhibited normal CD8+ T-cell expansion but reduced IFN-γ secretion, revealing a distinct binding–immunogenicity dissociation. MD simulations suggested that altered peptide conformational dynamics may contribute to differences in functional activity. Subsequently, an optimized six-peptide formulation (three APLs and three wild-type epitopes) was assembled. The resulting multi-epitope HPV-specific cytotoxic T lymphocytes (meHPV-CTLs) mediated target-specific cytotoxicity against cervical cancer cells, achieving 74.1% ± 5.1% specific lysis at an effector-to-target ratio of 30:1, which was largely abrogated by HLA class I blockade. Conclusions: These proof-of-concept findings demonstrate that stable peptide–MHC binding is a necessary but insufficient condition for optimal T-cell activation. The experimentally characterized multi-epitope formulation provides a proof-of-concept strategy for further preclinical evaluation of HPV16-targeted peptide-based immunotherapies. Moreover, because these anchor-optimized APLs are defined at the sequence level, these sequence-defined APLs may potentially be explored in alternative vaccine delivery platforms, including mRNA-based approaches, in future studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Therapeutic vaccination against HPV16 is constrained by poor presentation of native E6/E7 epitopes to HLA-A*02:01, creating a need for improved immunogens. The study sought to design anchor-optimized altered peptide ligands (APLs) that enhance peptide presentation and HPV16-specific T-cell responses.
Design and population
The study used an integrated computational-experimental pipeline to design and evaluate APLs targeting HPV16 E6/E7 epitopes. Anchor-residue substitutions were introduced into three wild-type epitopes and prioritized in silico before in vitro functional assays with HLA-A*02:01-positive donor cells and molecular dynamics simulations.
Core methods
Anchor-residue substitutions were introduced into three wild-type E6/E7 epitopes and candidates were prioritized in silico based on predicted presentation, affinity, immunogenicity, and toxicity. Prioritized candidates were evaluated via in vitro functional assays using HLA-A*02:01-positive donor cells and molecular dynamics simulations.
Main results
Anchor optimization converted weak binders into strong binders, with E6apl improving predicted affinity from 329.33 to 6.21 nM. Candidate E7apl1 showed normal CD8+ T-cell expansion but reduced IFN-γ secretion, indicating a binding–immunogenicity dissociation. The six-peptide formulation mediated 74.1% ± 5.1% specific lysis of cervical cancer cells at a 30:1 effector-to-target ratio, largely abrogated by HLA class I blockade.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that stable peptide–MHC binding is necessary but insufficient for optimal T-cell activation, highlighting a novel dissociation between binding and functional activity. The experimentally characterized multi-epitope formulation provides a proof-of-concept strategy for HPV16-targeted peptide-based immunotherapies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the rational design of APLs to enhance HPV16-specific CTL responses, potentially informing next-generation peptide-based vaccines or immunotherapies. Sequence-defined APLs may be adaptable to alternative delivery platforms, including mRNA-based approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to proof-of-concept in vitro validation without in vivo efficacy or safety data, reducing generalisability. Evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of clinical endpoints and reliance on computational predictions and ex vivo assays.

ARV-2001 的临床前开发,一种皮内注射 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒免疫疗法,用于治疗 HPV-16 阳性宫颈高级鳞状上皮内病变的 治疗

Preclinical Development of ARV-2001, an Intradermally Administered mRNA–Lipid Nanoparticle Immunotherapeutic for the Treatment of HPV-16-Positive Cervical High-Grade Squamous Intraepithelial Lesions
作者： Zhengxiang He, Huabin Zhu, Ju Hyeong Jeon, Jianzhu Chen, Gregory M. Glenn, Renhuan Xu
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:人乳头瘤病毒 16 型(HPV-16)持续感染是宫颈高级鳞状上皮内病变(cHSIL)和 宫颈癌 的主要原因,但已建立的 治疗 仍然仅限于具有生殖风险且不能消除潜在感染的消融或切除手术。我们报告了 ARV-2001 的临床前开发,ARV-2001 是一种信使 RNA (mRNA)-脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 免疫治疗药物,编码突变的非致癌性 HPV-16 E6 和 E7,与富含人类 CD4 辅助表位的 SARS-CoV-2 刺突 S2 子域融合,配制在新型胆固醇衍生的可电离脂质 (ARV-T1) 中。方法:在人宫颈癌细胞系 C33A、原代人角质形成细胞的慢病毒构建体以及软琼脂集落形成测定中评估 ARV-2001 表达抗原与 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤 (Rb) 的相互作用。ARV-2001 通过肌内 (IM) 或皮内 (ID) 注射方式给予幼鼠或 TC-1 肿瘤模型。随着时间的推移监测肿瘤大小和存活率,并通过流式细胞术表征肿瘤浸润的淋巴细胞。细胞内细胞因子染色和Elispot用于评估免疫原性。结果:在体外,突变的 E6/E7-S2 抗原失去了降解 p53、解除视网膜母细胞瘤 (Rb) 途径的调节以及支持不依赖贴壁生长的能力,表明致癌活性被消除。在同基因 HPV-16 E6/E7 TC-1 肿瘤模型中,S2 结构域和咪喹莫特给药均增强了抗肿瘤活性和瘤内 CD8+ T 细胞浸润,同时减少了骨髓源性抑制细胞。此外,在肿瘤生长抑制和存活方面,向TC-1荷瘤小鼠ID注射ARV-2001优于IM注射。ID 疫苗接种 与 ARV-2001 在小鼠体内持续引发比相同剂量的 IM 更有效的 E6/E7 特异性 T 细胞反应。剂量递增研究表明,ID 注射小鼠中针对 E6/E7 的 T 细胞反应呈剂量依赖性。结论:本研究支持未来在临床试验中对皮内注射 ARV-2001 对 HPV-16+ cHSIL 的 治疗 进行人体评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV-16) 的持续感染是宫颈高级鳞状上皮内病变 (cHSIL) 和 宫颈癌 的主要原因,但已建立的 治疗 仍然仅限于带有生殖风险的消融或切除手术,并且不能消除潜在的感染。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 ARV-2001(一种 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒免疫治疗药物)在幼稚小鼠和 TC-1 肿瘤模型(表达 HPV-16 E6/E7 的肿瘤模型)中的临床前开发。
核心方法
在人宫颈癌细胞系 C33A、原代人角质形成细胞的慢病毒构建体以及软琼脂集落形成测定中评估了 ARV-2001 表达抗原与 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤 (Rb) 的相互作用。ARV-2001 通过肌内 (IM) 或皮内 (ID) 注射方式给予幼鼠或 TC-1 肿瘤模型。随着时间的推移监测肿瘤大小和存活率,并通过流式细胞术表征肿瘤浸润的淋巴细胞。细胞内细胞因子染色和Elispot用于评估免疫原性。
主要结果
在体外,突变的 E6/E7-S2 抗原失去了降解 p53、解除视网膜母细胞瘤 (Rb) 途径的调节以及支持不依赖贴壁生长的能力,表明致癌活性被废除。在同基因 HPV-16 E6/E7 TC-1 肿瘤模型中,S2 结构域和咪喹莫特给药均增强了抗肿瘤活性和瘤内 CD8+ T 细胞浸润,同时减少了骨髓源性抑制细胞。此外,在肿瘤生长抑制和存活方面,向TC-1荷瘤小鼠ID注射ARV-2001优于IM注射。
解释与创新
研究表明,ARV-2001(一种 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒免疫治疗药物)可以消除 HPV-16 E6/E7 抗原的致癌活性,并增强 TC-1 肿瘤模型中的抗肿瘤 免疫应答。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,ARV-2001 的皮内给药可能为 HPV-16+ cHSIL 的 治疗 提供更有效的免疫治疗方法,可能减少侵入性操作的需要并改善患者的治疗结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于临床前模型,不包括人体试验,这降低了临床应用的证据强度。然而,多项体外和体内试验的结果是一致的,支持 ARV-2001 潜在功效的中等可信度。

Preclinical Development of ARV-2001, an Intradermally Administered mRNA–Lipid Nanoparticle Immunotherapeutic for the Treatment of HPV-16-Positive Cervical High-Grade Squamous Intraepithelial Lesions

Authors: Zhengxiang He, Huabin Zhu, Ju Hyeong Jeon, Jianzhu Chen, Gregory M. Glenn, Renhuan Xu
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objective: Persistent infection with human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV-16) is the principal cause of cervical high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (cHSIL) and cervical cancer, yet the established treatments remain limited to ablative or excisional procedures that carry reproductive risk and do not eliminate the underlying infection. We report the preclinical development of ARV-2001, a messenger RNA (mRNA)–lipid nanoparticle (LNP) immunotherapeutic encoding mutated, non-oncogenic HPV-16 E6 and E7 fused to a SARS-CoV-2 spike S2 subdomain enriched in human CD4 helper epitopes, formulated in a novel cholesterol-derived ionizable lipid (ARV-T1). Methods: Interactions of ARV-2001-expressed antigens with p53 and retinoblastoma (Rb) were evaluated in human cervical carcinoma cell line C33A, in lentiviral constructs in primary human keratinocytes, and in soft-agar colony-formation assays. ARV-2001 was administrated by intramuscular (IM) or intradermal (ID) injection in naive mice or in the TC-1 tumor models. Tumor size and survival were monitored over time and tumor-infiltrated lymphocytes were characterized by flow cytometry. Intracellular cytokine staining and Elispot were used to evaluate immunogenicity. Results: In vitro, the mutated E6/E7–S2 antigen lost the ability to degrade p53, to deregulate the retinoblastoma (Rb) pathway, and to support anchorage-independent growth, suggesting abrogation of oncogenic activity. The S2 domain and imiquimod administration each augmented antitumor activity and intratumoral CD8+ T-cell infiltration while reducing myeloid-derived suppressor cells in the syngeneic HPV-16 E6/E7 TC-1 tumor models. In addition, ID administration of ARV-2001 into TC-1 tumor-bearing mice was superior to IM administration in terms of both tumor growth inhibition and survival. ID vaccination with ARV-2001 in mice consistently elicited a more potent E6/E7-specific T-cell response than the same dose given IM. Dose-escalation studies showed a dose-dependent T cell response against E6/E7 in ID-injected mice. Conclusions: This study supports future human evaluation of intradermally administrated ARV-2001 for treatment of HPV-16+ cHSIL in clinical trials.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV-16) is the principal cause of cervical high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (cHSIL) and cervical cancer, yet the established treatments remain limited to ablative or excisional procedures that carry reproductive risk and do not eliminate the underlying infection.
Design and population
The study involved preclinical development of ARV-2001, an mRNA–lipid nanoparticle immunotherapeutic, in naive mice and in the TC-1 tumor models, which are HPV-16 E6/E7-expressing tumor models.
Core methods
Interactions of ARV-2001-expressed antigens with p53 and retinoblastoma (Rb) were evaluated in human cervical carcinoma cell line C33A, in lentiviral constructs in primary human keratinocytes, and in soft-agar colony-formation assays. ARV-2001 was administrated by intramuscular (IM) or intradermal (ID) injection in naive mice or in the TC-1 tumor models. Tumor size and survival were monitored over time and tumor-infiltrated lymphocytes were characterized by flow cytometry. Intracellular cytokine staining and Elispot were used to evaluate immunogenicity.
Main results
In vitro, the mutated E6/E7–S2 antigen lost the ability to degrade p53, to deregulate the retinoblastoma (Rb) pathway, and to support anchorage-independent growth, suggesting abrogation of oncogenic activity. The S2 domain and imiquimod administration each augmented antitumor activity and intratumoral CD8+ T-cell infiltration while reducing myeloid-derived suppressor cells in the syngeneic HPV-16 E6/E7 TC-1 tumor models. In addition, ID administration of ARV-2001 into TC-1 tumor-bearing mice was superior to IM administration in terms of both tumor growth inhibition and survival.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that ARV-2001, an mRNA–lipid nanoparticle immunotherapeutic, can abrogate oncogenic activity of HPV-16 E6/E7 antigens and enhance antitumor immune responses in TC-1 tumor models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that intradermal administration of ARV-2001 may offer a more effective immunotherapeutic approach for the treatment of HPV-16+ cHSIL, potentially reducing the need for invasive procedures and improving patient outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to preclinical models and does not include human trials, which reduces the strength of evidence for clinical application. However, the results are consistent across multiple in vitro and in vivo assays, supporting a moderate confidence level in the potential efficacy of ARV-2001.

中国男大学生跨境接受人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种的意愿:统计间接关联分析的横断面研究

Willingness to receive cross-border human papillomavirus vaccination among Chinese male university students: a cross-sectional study with statistical indirect-association analysis
作者： Yunyu Zhang, Wenming Cao, Hongyi Jiang, Jianyu Wan, Ya Liu, Huiyan Liao, Mei Wang, Weiwei Sun, Yiran Fu, Lu Cui
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 是预防男性 HPV 相关疾病的重要策略。
设计与对象
2025年9月至2026年3月,对中国大陆、香港、澳门和海外的4,260名男大学生进行了一项横断面匿名在线调查。
核心方法
使用与 HPV 相关的四种疾病来评估 HPV 相关疾病的识别:肛门生殖器疣、阴茎癌、肛门癌和口咽癌。多变量逻辑回归估计调整后的比值比 (aOR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI)。
主要结果
Of 4,260 participants, 3,182 (74.69%) reported willingness to receive cross-border HPV vaccination. Participants with a disease-recognition gap had substantially lower adjusted odds of willingness than those identifying at least one condition (aOR = 0.041, 95% CI: 0.017–0.095; p < 0.001). Awareness of cross-border HPV vaccination availability was positively associated with willingness (aOR = 1.233, 95% CI: 1.056–1.441; p = 0.008).
解释与创新
对已确定的 HPV 相关男性疾病的认识以及对跨境 疫苗接种 可用性的认识与意愿相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,提高对跨境 疫苗接种 选项的疾病认识和认识可能会提高中国男大学生接受 HPV 疫苗接种 的意愿。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计排除了因果推断。

Willingness to receive cross-border human papillomavirus vaccination among Chinese male university students: a cross-sectional study with statistical indirect-association analysis

Authors: Yunyu Zhang, Wenming Cao, Hongyi Jiang, Jianyu Wan, Ya Liu, Huiyan Liao, Mei Wang, Weiwei Sun, Yiran Fu, Lu Cui
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is an important strategy for preventing HPV-related disease among males. Access to male HPV vaccination may involve cross-border health seeking for some university students in mainland China. This study estimated willingness to receive cross-border HPV vaccination among Chinese male university students and examined associations of HPV-related disease recognition, awareness of cross-border vaccination availability, and demographic and informational characteristics with willingness. Methods A cross-sectional anonymous online survey was conducted between September 2025 and March 2026 among 4,260 male university students in mainland China, Hong Kong, Macao, and overseas. The primary outcome was willingness to travel at personal expense to Hong Kong or Macao for HPV vaccination. HPV-related disease recognition was assessed using four conditions with established HPV associations: anogenital warts, penile cancer, anal cancer, and oropharyngeal cancer. Multivariable logistic regression estimated adjusted odds ratios (aORs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Results Of 4,260 participants, 3,182 (74.69%) reported willingness to receive cross-border HPV vaccination. Participants with a disease-recognition gap had substantially lower adjusted odds of willingness than those identifying at least one condition (aOR = 0.041, 95% CI: 0.017–0.095; p < 0.001). Awareness of cross-border HPV vaccination availability was positively associated with willingness (aOR = 1.233, 95% CI: 1.056–1.441; p = 0.008). Students studying in Guangdong province had higher adjusted odds of willingness than students in other mainland provinces (aOR = 1.213, 95% CI: 1.045–1.409; p = 0.011). Higher academic grade (aOR per level = 1.111, 95% CI: 1.023–1.206; p = 0.012) and exposure to school-based HPV information (aOR = 1.203, 95% CI: 1.005–1.440; p = 0.044) were also independently associated with willingness. The estimated indirect association through cross-border service awareness was statistically significant but small ( B = −0.022, bootstrap 95% CI: −0.040 to −0.006). Conclusion Willingness to receive cross-border HPV vaccination was reported by approximately three-quarters of participants. Recognition of established HPV-related male diseases and awareness of cross-border vaccination availability were associated with willingness. The cross-sectional design precludes causal inference. Longitudinal and intervention studies are needed to determine whether male-focused HPV education and service-navigation strategies increase actual vaccine uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is an important strategy for preventing HPV-related disease among males.
Design and population
A cross-sectional anonymous online survey was conducted between September 2025 and March 2026 among 4,260 male university students in mainland China, Hong Kong, Macao, and overseas.
Core methods
HPV-related disease recognition was assessed using four conditions with established HPV associations: anogenital warts, penile cancer, anal cancer, and oropharyngeal cancer. Multivariable logistic regression estimated adjusted odds ratios (aORs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs).
Main results
Of 4,260 participants, 3,182 (74.69%) reported willingness to receive cross-border HPV vaccination. Participants with a disease-recognition gap had substantially lower adjusted odds of willingness than those identifying at least one condition (aOR = 0.041, 95% CI: 0.017–0.095; p < 0.001). Awareness of cross-border HPV vaccination availability was positively associated with willingness (aOR = 1.233, 95% CI: 1.056–1.441; p = 0.008).
Interpretation and novelty
Recognition of established HPV-related male diseases and awareness of cross-border vaccination availability were associated with willingness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving disease recognition and awareness of cross-border vaccination options may enhance willingness to receive HPV vaccination among Chinese male university students.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design precludes causal inference.

Human Papillomavirus Vaccination and Cervical Cancer Rates Since 2018: A Statistical Analysis

Authors: Ashley Hoover, Alexandra Taylor, Katherine Fuller
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human Papillomavirus (HPV) is a common sexually transmitted virus that is the primary cause of cervical cancer. Most cervical cancer cases are caused by HPV strains that we currently have a vaccine against. In May 2018, the Director-General of the World Health Organization (WHO) announced a global call to action towards the elimination of cervical cancer.

p16/Ki-67双染色在高危型HPV阳性女性宫颈病变筛查中的临床应用分析

Analysis of the clinical utility of p16/Ki-67 dual staining in screening cervical lesions among women positive for high-risk HPV

问题与背景
宫颈癌 与高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的持续感染密切相关。尽管 HPV 检测具有高灵敏度,但其有限的特异性可能会导致过多的阴道镜检查转诊,并且传统液基细胞学 (TCT) 的分类性能在 hrHPV 阳性女性中仍然不够理想。
设计与对象
回顾性地,这项单中心 队列研究 研究纳入了 2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 10 月期间到我们诊所就诊的 100 名连续 hrHPV 阳性女性。在纳入的 100 名 hrHPV 阳性女性中,35 名 (35.0%) 被诊断患有宫颈上皮内瘤变 2+ (CIN2+)。
核心方法
所有参与者在同一次就诊期间均接受了 hrHPV 基因分型、TCT 和 p16/Ki-67 双重染色,然后进行阴道镜检查和组织病理学评估。组织学作为参考标准。我们评估了 p16/Ki-67 对 CIN2+ 病变的诊断性能,并将其与 TCT、HPV 基因分型和组合分类模型(hrHPV + TCT + p16/Ki-67)进行比较。
主要结果

解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,p16/Ki-67 双重染色可以提高 hrHPV 阳性女性宫颈病变分类的准确性,有可能减少不必要的阴道镜检查转诊并优化筛查策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单中心设计和回顾性而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。该证据是中等的,因为它基于样本量相对较小的单个 队列研究 研究。

Analysis of the clinical utility of p16/Ki-67 dual staining in screening cervical lesions among women positive for high-risk HPV

Authors: Qingsong Zhang, Yunpeng Lin, Leilei Bao, Qiuyang Huang
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is closely associated with persistent infection by high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV). Although HPV testing offers high sensitivity, its limited specificity may result in excessive colposcopy referrals, and the triage performance of traditional liquid-based cytology (TCT) remains suboptimal among hrHPV-positive women. As a biomarker reflecting HPV-mediated cell-cycle dysregulation and aberrant proliferation, p16/Ki-67 dual staining has shown potential advantages in identifying clinically significant high-grade cervical lesions. This study aimed to evaluate the clinical utility of p16/Ki-67 dual staining in screening cervical lesions among hrHPV-positive women and to explore its role in optimizing referral strategies. Retrospectively, this single-center cohort study included 100 consecutive hrHPV-positive women who attended our clinic between October 2024 and October 2025. All participants underwent hrHPV genotyping, TCT, and p16/Ki-67 dual staining during the same visit, followed by colposcopy and histopathological assessment. Histology served as the reference standard. We evaluated the diagnostic performance of p16/Ki-67 for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ (CIN2+) lesions and compared it with TCT, HPV genotyping, and a combined triage model (hrHPV + TCT + p16/Ki-67). In addition, we simulated the screening efficiency of different colposcopy referral strategies. Among the 100 hrHPV-positive women included, 35 (35.0%) were diagnosed with CIN2+. The prevalence of HPV16 infection and the positivity rate of p16/Ki-67 dual staining were significantly higher in the CIN2+ group than in the non-CIN2+ group ( P

Question and background
Cervical cancer is closely associated with persistent infection by high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV). Although HPV testing offers high sensitivity, its limited specificity may result in excessive colposcopy referrals, and the triage performance of traditional liquid-based cytology (TCT) remains suboptimal among hrHPV-positive women.
Design and population
Retrospectively, this single-center cohort study included 100 consecutive hrHPV-positive women who attended our clinic between October 2024 and October 2025. Among the 100 hrHPV-positive women included, 35 (35.0%) were diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ (CIN2+).
Core methods
All participants underwent hrHPV genotyping, TCT, and p16/Ki-67 dual staining during the same visit, followed by colposcopy and histopathological assessment. Histology served as the reference standard. We evaluated the diagnostic performance of p16/Ki-67 for CIN2+ lesions and compared it with TCT, HPV genotyping, and a combined triage model (hrHPV + TCT + p16/Ki-67).
Main results
The prevalence of HPV16 infection and the positivity rate of p16/Ki-67 dual staining were significantly higher in the CIN2+ group than in the non-CIN2+ group (P.
Interpretation and novelty
As a biomarker reflecting HPV-mediated cell-cycle dysregulation and aberrant proliferation, p16/Ki-67 dual staining has shown potential advantages in identifying clinically significant high-grade cervical lesions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that p16/Ki-67 dual staining may improve the accuracy of triage for cervical lesions in hrHPV-positive women, potentially reducing unnecessary colposcopy referrals and optimizing screening strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design and retrospective nature, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single cohort study with a relatively small sample size.

使用人工深度神经网络对人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 进行分数阶数学建模

Fractional-Order Mathematical Modeling of Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) Using Artificial Deep Neural Networks
作者： Sadique Ahmad, Eiman, Altaf Khan, Ala Saleh Alluhaidan, Mohammed A. Elaffendi
摘要中文翻译该研究提出了一个数学框架来评估 HPV 感染 传播 动态,同时考虑到与分数阶微积分相关的先进方法。HPV 感染和 宫颈癌 始终是世界各地的主要公共卫生威胁,特别是在那些医疗保健服务和控制干预措施不足的地区。第一步,精确检查解的正性和有界性,以表明该模型从长远来看仍然具有生物学价值。在本研究中,提供无病平衡和地方病平衡来描述疾病在人群中的长期行为。还研究了基本繁殖数和敏感性分析,因为它对于分析动力学行为至关重要。理论结果是使用不动点理论为所考虑的模型建立的。接下来,清楚地证明了 Hyers-Ulam (H-U) 型稳定性的条件。然后,应用修正欧拉方法(MEM)计算所提出模型的数值解。最后,我们给出了所涉及结果的图形表示。我们对我们考虑的模型中涉及的参数使用一些实际值。使用Matlab进行图形演示。通过全面的数值分析,该模型探讨了各种分数阶值如何影响 HPV 传播 的动态。因此,揭示了分数阶如何更好地传达社区内复杂关系的建议。这些进展也让人们认识到该方法在推进疾病建模和加深我们对 HPV 传播 的了解方面的重要性。在人工深度神经网络 (DNN) 的帮助下,我们研究了所考虑的模型。我们通过渐进分析推导出一些统计阈值,包括均方误差(MSE)和均方根误差(RMSE)。此外,还使用不同的分数阶对受感染类别进行了图示。对不同分数阶的模拟结果和 DNN 预测结果之间的绝对误差进行了比较。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究提出了一个使用分数阶微积分评估 HPV 传播 动态的数学框架,解决了 HPV 感染和相关癌症造成的持续公共卫生威胁,特别是在医疗保健获取和控制措施有限的地区。
设计与对象
该研究开发了 HPV 感染 传播 动力学的理论数学模型,检查无病和地方性平衡以描述长期行为,而无需指定人群或临床环境。
核心方法
作者使用不动点理论来建立理论结果,应用改进的欧拉方法进行数值解,并使用人工深度神经网络(DNN)来研究模型,包括计算均方误差(MSE)和均方根误差(RMSE)进行验证。
主要结果
该研究证明了模型的 Hyers-Ulam (H-U) 类型稳定性条件,并提供了不同分数阶的感染类别的图形表示,比较了模拟值和 DNN 预测值之间的绝对误差。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为揭示了分数阶值如何影响 HPV 传播 动态,表明分数阶微积分可以更好地捕捉复杂的群落关系,并强调该方法在推进疾病建模方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,分数阶数学模型与人工神经网络相结合可以提高对 HPV 传播 模式的理解,可能为资源有限环境中的 监测 和控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
这些证据只是理论和抽象的,缺乏在人群或临床数据中的实证验证,这限制了其对公共卫生实践的直接适用性,并需要进行低置信度评估。

Fractional-Order Mathematical Modeling of Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) Using Artificial Deep Neural Networks

Authors: Sadique Ahmad, Eiman, Altaf Khan, Ala Saleh Alluhaidan, Mohammed A. Elaffendi
Original abstract (English)
The study proposes a mathematical framework to evaluate the HPV infection transmission dynamics, taking into account advanced approaches related to fractional calculus. The HPV infection and cervical cancer is always a major public health threat across the world, particularly in those areas having insufficient availability to health care services and control interventions. In first step, the positivity and boundedness of solutions are precisely examined to show that the model remains biologically valuable in the long run. In this study the disease free equilibrium and endemic equilibrium is provided to describe the long term behavior of the disease in a population. The basic reproductive number and sensitivity analysis is also studied as it is vital to analyse behavior of the dynamics. The theoretical results is establish for the model under consideration using fixed point theory. Next, the condition for Hyers-Ulam’s (H-U) type stability is clearly demonstrated. Then, applying the modified Euler’s method (MEM) for the computation of numerical solution of the proposed model. At the end, we give graphical representation of the involved results. We use some real values for the parameters involved in our considered model. Matlab is used for graphical demonstration. Through a comprehensive numerical analysis, the model explores how various fractional order values inuence the dynamics of HPV transmission. Hence, revealing suggestions for how fractional order may better convey the intricate relationships within the communities. These development also draw awareness to the method’s significance in advancing disease modeling and deepening our knowledge of HPV transmission. With the help of artificial deep neural networks (DNNs), we investigate the considered model. We deduced some statistical thresholds including mean squared error (MSE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) together with progressional analysis. Further, graphical illustrations are given for infected classes using different fractional order. Absolute error between the simulated and those predicted by DNNs are compared for various fractional orders.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the persistent public health threat posed by HPV infection and associated cancers, particularly in regions with limited healthcare access and control measures, by proposing a mathematical framework to evaluate HPV transmission dynamics using fractional calculus.
Design and population
The study develops a theoretical mathematical model of HPV infection transmission dynamics, examining disease-free and endemic equilibria to describe long-term behavior without specifying a human population or clinical setting.
Core methods
The authors use fixed point theory to establish theoretical results, apply the modified Euler’s method for numerical solutions, and employ artificial deep neural networks (DNNs) to investigate the model, including computation of mean squared error (MSE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) for validation.
Main results
The study demonstrates Hyers-Ulam’s (H-U) type stability conditions for the model and provides graphical representations of infected classes across different fractional orders, comparing absolute errors between simulated and DNN-predicted values.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as revealing how fractional order values influence HPV transmission dynamics, suggesting that fractional calculus may better capture complex community relationships, and highlight the method’s significance in advancing disease modeling.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings imply that fractional-order mathematical modeling combined with artificial neural networks could improve understanding of HPV transmission patterns, potentially informing surveillance and control strategies in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is theoretical and abstract-only, lacking empirical validation in human populations or clinical data, which limits its direct applicability to public health practice and warrants a low confidence assessment.

土耳其女性中人乳头瘤病毒的 患病率

The Prevalence of Human Papilloma Virus in Turkish Women
作者： Göktürk Han DOĞAN, Ceren Sağlam, İbrahim Gülhan, Ilayda Gercik, Abdurrahman Hamdi İnan, Muzaffer Şancı, Rumeysa Nur Çağıran, Yunus Emre Purut, Ceren Sağlam Purut, İlayda Gercik Arzık
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:土耳其人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的 患病率 可能与其他国家报告的有所不同。在大样本量的支持下,确定所有测试个体中 HPV 感染的年龄特定分布及其总体 患病率,将为 HPV 预防计划提供宝贵的见解。本研究旨在确定HPV感染的区域患病率。方法:这是一项回顾性研究,包含 2016 年至 2024 年间在三级医院接受 HPV 检测的 52,930 名患者的数据。研究包括年龄 21 至 65 岁的患者。通过聚合酶链式反应(PCR)和核酸杂交技术进行目标DNA扩增以鉴定HPV。检查了总体 患病率、年龄特异性 患病率 和基因型分布。结果:基因型分布分析显示,单独的 HPV16 阳性率为 4.0%(n = 2118),单独的 HPV18 阳性率为 1.0%(n = 528),在 10.8%(n = 5693)的患者中检测到单独的其他高危(HR)HPV 类型(不包括 HPV16 和 HPV18)。共阳性率如下:HPV16和HPV18为0.4%(n = 237),HPV16和HR-HPV其他为2.3%(n = 1197),HPV18和HR-HPV其他为1.0%(n = 513),HPV16、HPV18和HR-HPV其他为0.3%(n = 145)。结论:本研究强调,我国 21-24 岁人群中 HPV 阳性率较高,总体 患病率 与之前土耳其和国际研究报告的范围大致一致,并且汇总的其他 HR-HPV 组比 HPV16 或 HPV18 单独出现的频率更高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了描述土耳其 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的 患病率 特征的需要,并指出报告的 患病率 可能与其他国家不同,并且特定年龄的分布数据对于预防计划很有价值。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了 2016 年至 2024 年间在三级医院接受 HPV 检测的 52,930 名 21 至 65 岁患者的数据。
核心方法
HPV 鉴定是通过聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 和核酸杂交技术使用靶 DNA 扩增进行的。检查了总体 患病率、年龄特异性 患病率 和基因型分布。
主要结果
单独的 HPV16 阳性率为 4.0% (n = 2118),单独的 HPV18 阳性率为 1.0% (n = 528),在 10.8% (n = 5693) 的患者中检测到单独的其他高危 (HR) HPV 类型(不包括 HPV16 和 HPV18)。共阳性率包括 HPV16 和 HPV18 为 0.4%(n = 237),HPV16 和 HR-HPV 其他为 2.3%(n = 1197),HPV18 和 HR-HPV 其他为 1.0%(n = 513),HPV16、HPV18 和 HR-HPV 其他为 0.3%(n = 145)。
解释与创新
该研究解释说,21-24 岁个体中 HPV 阳性率较高,总体 患病率 与之前的土耳其和国际报告一致,并且汇总的其他 HR-HPV 组比 HPV16 或 HPV18 单独出现的频率更高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过提供区域 患病率 数据和特定年龄的分布模式来指导 HPV 预防计划,以指导土耳其的 疫苗接种 和筛查策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计、单中心设置以及依赖基于 PCR 的检测而没有临床结果数据,从而限制了普遍性和因果推断;证据强度中等。

The Prevalence of Human Papilloma Virus in Turkish Women

Authors: Göktürk Han DOĞAN, Ceren Sağlam, İbrahim Gülhan, Ilayda Gercik, Abdurrahman Hamdi İnan, Muzaffer Şancı, Rumeysa Nur Çağıran, Yunus Emre Purut, Ceren Sağlam Purut, İlayda Gercik Arzık
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: The prevalence of Human Papilloma Virus (HPV) in Türkiye may vary from that reported in other countries. Determining the age-specific distribution of HPV infection and its overall prevalence in all tested individuals, supported by a large sample size, would provide valuable insights for HPV prevention programs. This study aims to determine the regional prevalence of HPV infection. Methods: This is a retrospective study containing data from 52,930 patients who underwent HPV testing at a tertiary hospital between 2016 and 2024. Patients aged 21 to 65 were included in the study. Target DNA amplification by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and nucleic acid hybridization techniques were performed for the identification of HPV. Overall prevalence, age-specific prevalence, and genotype distribution were examined. Results: Analysis of genotype distribution revealed that isolated HPV16 positivity was 4.0% (n = 2118), isolated HPV18 positivity was 1.0% (n = 528), and isolated other high-risk (HR) HPV types (excluding HPV16 and HPV18) were detected in 10.8% (n = 5693) of patients. The co-positivity rates were as follows: HPV16 and HPV18 in 0.4% (n = 237), HPV16 and HR-HPV other in 2.3% (n = 1197), HPV18 and HR-HPV other in 1.0% (n = 513), and HPV16, HPV18, and HR-HPV other in 0.3% (n = 145). Conclusions: This study highlights that HPV positivity is higher among individuals aged 21–24 years in our population, the overall prevalence is broadly consistent with the range reported in previous Turkish and international studies, and the pooled other HR-HPV group was more frequent than HPV16 or HPV18 individually.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to characterize the prevalence of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) in Türkiye, noting that reported prevalence may differ from other countries and that age-specific distribution data are valuable for prevention programs.
Design and population
This retrospective study analyzed data from 52,930 patients aged 21 to 65 who underwent HPV testing at a tertiary hospital between 2016 and 2024.
Core methods
HPV identification was performed using target DNA amplification by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and nucleic acid hybridization techniques. Overall prevalence, age-specific prevalence, and genotype distribution were examined.
Main results
Isolated HPV16 positivity was 4.0% (n = 2118), isolated HPV18 positivity was 1.0% (n = 528), and isolated other high-risk (HR) HPV types (excluding HPV16 and HPV18) were detected in 10.8% (n = 5693) of patients. Co-positivity rates included HPV16 and HPV18 in 0.4% (n = 237), HPV16 and HR-HPV other in 2.3% (n = 1197), HPV18 and HR-HPV other in 1.0% (n = 513), and HPV16, HPV18, and HR-HPV other in 0.3% (n = 145).
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets that HPV positivity is higher among individuals aged 21–24 years, the overall prevalence aligns with prior Turkish and international reports, and the pooled other HR-HPV group was more frequent than HPV16 or HPV18 individually.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform HPV prevention programs by providing regional prevalence data and age-specific distribution patterns to guide vaccination and screening strategies in Türkiye.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, single-centre setting, and reliance on PCR-based testing without clinical outcome data, which constrains generalisability and causal inference; evidence strength is moderate.

衣壳中的 人乳头瘤病毒 L2 蛋白是准备离开病毒颗粒的相关结构的集合

The human papillomavirus L2 protein in the capsid is an ensemble of related structures poised to exit the virus particle
作者： Changin Oh, Huaxin Yu, Patrick M Buckley, Frank L Horrigan, Kashif Mehmood, Jeongjoon Choi, Jun Liu, Daniel DiMaio
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 衣壳包含 360 个 L1 蛋白分子(可组装成形成衣壳壳的五聚体)和最多 72 个 L2 蛋白分子。L2 蛋白的 C 末端必须从衣壳中出现,插入内体膜,并突出到细胞质中,以与在病毒进入过程中介导病毒运输到细胞核的细胞蛋白结合。计算分析预测 L2 的大部分是无序的,但衣壳中 L2 的化学计量、结构和位置仍然难以捉摸。在这里,我们使用冷冻电镜单粒子分析含有和不含有 L2 蛋白的 HPV16 假病毒衣壳,并结合 AlphaFold3 预测和分子动力学模拟,开发衣壳内 L2 的结构模型。我们的结果表明,L2 的单个分子与大多数 L1 五聚体的内表面结合。五聚体相对刚性的结构迫使L2的长的本质上无序的片段在衣壳中采用许多相关的、紧凑的、稳定的构象,而L2的结构元件保持折叠,但在主要位于五聚体下方的空间中采用不同的位置。L2 的手指状突起延伸到五聚体的中心孔中,但 L2 无序结构域的许多不同片段可以形成手指。在一些模型中,L2 的 C 末端穿过孔延伸至衣壳的外表面,定位为插入内体膜。与这些模型一致,对纯化的假病毒的分析表明,多个 L2 分子的 C 末端暴露在每个衣壳的表面。这些发现为理解 L2 在衣壳中如何组织以在病毒进入期间启动其作用提供了结构基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解 HPV 衣壳内 L2 蛋白的结构组织及其在病毒进入中的作用。背景强调了 L2 蛋白在病毒进入细胞核过程中介导转运的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究使用冷冻电镜单颗粒分析含有和不含 L2 蛋白的 HPV16 假病毒衣壳,并结合 AlphaFold3 预测和分子动力学模拟。分析重点是衣壳内 L2 的结构模型。
核心方法
这些方法包括 HPV16 假病毒衣壳的冷冻电镜单粒子分析、蛋白质结构的 AlphaFold3 预测以及衣壳内 L2 蛋白质构象变化的分子动力学模拟。
主要结果
结果表明,单个 L2 分子与大多数 L1 五聚体的内表面缔合。此外,发现多个 L2 分子的 C 末端暴露在每个衣壳的表面。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,L2 蛋白在衣壳中的组织方式使其能够在病毒进入过程中启动其作用,为理解其在此过程中的作用提供了结构基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解 HPV 衣壳结构和 L2 蛋白的功能,这可能为未来有关病毒进入机制的研究和靶向治疗或 疫苗 的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于计算分析和冷冻电镜,在捕获 L2 构象变化的完整动态方面可能存在局限性。然而,证据有多种方法支持,强度中等。

The human papillomavirus L2 protein in the capsid is an ensemble of related structures poised to exit the virus particle

Authors: Changin Oh, Huaxin Yu, Patrick M Buckley, Frank L Horrigan, Kashif Mehmood, Jeongjoon Choi, Jun Liu, Daniel DiMaio
Original abstract (English)
The human papillomavirus (HPV) capsid contains 360 molecules of the L1 protein, which assembles into pentamers that form the capsid shell, and up to 72 molecules of the L2 protein. The C-terminus of the L2 protein must emerge from the capsid, insert into the endosome membrane, and protrude into the cytoplasm to bind to cellular proteins that mediate trafficking of the virus to the nucleus during virus entry. Computational analysis predicts that much of L2 is disordered, but the stoichiometry, structure and location of L2 in the capsid remain elusive. Here, we use cryo-EM single particle analysis of HPV16 pseudovirus capsids with and without the L2 protein combined with AlphaFold3 predictions and molecular dynamics simulations to develop a structural model of L2 within the capsid. Our results revealed that a single molecule of L2 associates with the inner surface of most L1 pentamers. The relatively rigid structure of the pentamer forces the long intrinsically disordered segments of L2 to adopt numerous related, compact, stable conformations in the capsid, whereas the structured elements of L2 remain folded but adopt varied positions in space largely beneath the pentamer. A finger-like projection of L2 extends into the central pore of the pentamer, but many different segments of the disordered domains of L2 can form fingers. In some models the C-terminus of L2 extends through the pore to the exterior surface of the capsid positioned to insert into the endosomal membrane. Consistent with these models, analysis of purified pseudoviruses showed that the C-terminus of multiple L2 molecules is exposed on the surface of each capsid. These findings provide a structural basis for understanding how L2 is organized in the capsid poised to initiate its action during virus entry.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the structural organization of the L2 protein within the HPV capsid and its role in virus entry. The background highlights the importance of the L2 protein in mediating trafficking of the virus to the nucleus during entry.
Design and population
The study used cryo-EM single particle analysis of HPV16 pseudovirus capsids with and without the L2 protein, combined with AlphaFold3 predictions and molecular dynamics simulations. The analysis focused on the structural model of L2 within the capsid.
Core methods
The methods involved cryo-EM single particle analysis of HPV16 pseudovirus capsids, AlphaFold3 predictions for protein structure, and molecular dynamics simulations to model the conformational changes of the L2 protein within the capsid.
Main results
The results showed that a single molecule of L2 associates with the inner surface of most L1 pentamers. Additionally, the C-terminus of multiple L2 molecules was found to be exposed on the surface of each capsid.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the L2 protein is organized in the capsid in a way that allows it to initiate its action during virus entry, providing a structural basis for understanding its role in the process.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to the understanding of HPV capsid structure and the function of the L2 protein, which may inform future studies on virus entry mechanisms and the development of targeted therapies or vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on computational analysis and cryo-EM, which may have limitations in capturing the full dynamics of L2 conformational changes. However, the evidence is supported by multiple methods and is considered moderate in strength.

人乳头瘤病毒 16型基因多态性、致病性及持续感染能力调查

Investigation of Human papillomavirus Type 16’s genetic polymorphism, pathogenicity, and persistent infection capacity
作者： Sarah Abbas Fadhil, Qin Liu
摘要中文翻译方法:招募了在伊拉克巴格达三家医院(Al-Alawiyah 医院、Al-Habibyah 医院和巴格达教学医院)就诊的女性受试者。结果:在分析的 3,453 个样本中,974 人检测到 HPV 感染。致谢我们衷心感谢参与本研究的研究团队的所有研究人员。摘要 背景 有限的研究探索了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 16 型的基因多态性、病毒载量、适应性、持续性和致病性之间的关系。了解这些关联对于阐明 HPV16 致病机制和改进预防、筛查和控制策略至关重要。收集脱落的宫颈细胞样本。使用实时荧光聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 进行 HPV 基因分型和病毒载量定量,并使用桑格测序对 E6 和 E7 癌基因进行测序。分析重点关注 HPV16 检测的时间趋势、HPV16 谱系和亚谱系 (A–D) 的分布以及 E6/E7 序列变异。还评估了病毒谱系、病毒载量、致病性和持久性之间的关联。在 162 例 HPV 阳性病例中鉴定出 HPV16,总体 患病率 为 6.16%。2022 年至 2025 年间,观察到 HPV16 检出率存在显着差异。HPV16 谱系 A 原型的 E6 和 E7 序列与参考 基因组 相同,是最常见的谱系。这种显性谱系与持续感染和宫颈疾病(包括宫颈上皮内瘤变和 宫颈癌)的风险增加相关。疾病组和无病变组之间以及持续感染组和病毒清除组之间的病毒载量存在显着差异。无显着关联...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
有限的研究探索了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 16 型的基因多态性、病毒载量、适应性、持久性和致病性之间的关系,造成了关于其致病机制和临床意义的知识空白。
设计与对象
招募了在伊拉克巴格达三家医院(Al-Alawiyah 医院、Al-Habibyah 医院和巴格达教学医院)就诊的女性受试者,并收集脱落的宫颈细胞样本进行分析。
核心方法
使用实时荧光聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 进行 HPV 基因分型和病毒载量定量,并使用桑格测序对 E6 和 E7 癌基因进行测序,以评估 HPV16 检测、谱系分布以及病毒谱系、病毒载量、致病性和持久性之间的关联的时间趋势。
主要结果
在分析的 3,453 个样本中,在 162 例 HPV 阳性病例中鉴定出 HPV16,总体 患病率 为 6.16%,2022 年至 2025 年 HPV16 检出率存在显着差异。HPV16 谱系 A 原型最为普遍,与持续感染和宫颈疾病(包括宫颈上皮内瘤变和 宫颈癌)风险增加相关。疾病组和无病变组之间以及持续感染组和病毒清除组之间的病毒载量存在显着差异,但病毒载量与 E6/E7 突变(E6 L83V 或 E7 N29S)之间没有观察到显着关联。
解释与创新
作者解释说,HPV16 谱系 A 原型在病毒致病性和持久性中发挥着关键作用,并且病毒载量与疾病严重程度和持久性呈正相关,这表明其作为临床生物标志物的潜在用途。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能通过强调 A 谱系在持续感染和疾病进展中的重要性,为 HPV16 监测 策略提供信息,并且可以支持使用病毒载量作为临床环境中风险分层的生物标志物。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注单一地理区域,并且需要进一步研究以阐明 HPV16 进化 和适应的其他分子机制,需要中等强度的证据。

Investigation of Human papillomavirus Type 16’s genetic polymorphism, pathogenicity, and persistent infection capacity

Authors: Sarah Abbas Fadhil, Qin Liu
Original abstract (English)
Methods: Female subjects attending three hospitals in Baghdad, Iraq—Al-Alawiyah Hospital, Al-Habibyah Hospital, and Baghdad Teaching Hospital—were enrolled. Results: Of 3,453 samples analyzed, HPV infection was detected in 974 individuals. Acknowledgements We sincerely thank all the researchers of our research team involved in this study. Abstract Background Limited research has explored the relationships among genetic polymorphisms, viral load, adaptability, persistence, and pathogenicity of human papillomavirus (HPV) type 16. Understanding these associations is essential for clarifying HPV16 pathogenic mechanisms and improving prevention, screening, and control strategies. Exfoliated cervical cell samples were collected. HPV genotyping and viral load quantification were performed using real-time fluorescence polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and the E6 and E7 oncogenes were sequenced using Sanger sequencing. Analyses focused on temporal trends in HPV16 detection, distribution of HPV16 lineages and sublineages (A–D), and E6/E7 sequence variants. Associations between viral lineage, viral load, pathogenicity, and persistence were also evaluated. HPV16 was identified in 162 HPV-positive cases, yielding an overall prevalence of 6.16%. Significant differences in HPV16 detection rates were observed between 2022 and 2025. The HPV16 lineage A prototype, with E6 and E7 sequences identical to the reference genome, was the most prevalent lineage. This dominant lineage was associated with an increased risk of persistent infection and cervical disease, including cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and cervical cancer. Viral load differed significantly between disease and lesion-free groups, as well as between persistent infection and viral clearance groups. No significant associatio...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Limited research has explored the relationships among genetic polymorphisms, viral load, adaptability, persistence, and pathogenicity of human papillomavirus (HPV) type 16, creating a knowledge gap regarding its pathogenic mechanisms and clinical implications.
Design and population
Female subjects attending three hospitals in Baghdad, Iraq—Al-Alawiyah Hospital, Al-Habibyah Hospital, and Baghdad Teaching Hospital—were enrolled, and exfoliated cervical cell samples were collected for analysis.
Core methods
HPV genotyping and viral load quantification were performed using real-time fluorescence polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and the E6 and E7 oncogenes were sequenced using Sanger sequencing to evaluate temporal trends in HPV16 detection, lineage distribution, and associations between viral lineage, viral load, pathogenicity, and persistence.
Main results
Of 3,453 samples analyzed, HPV16 was identified in 162 HPV-positive cases, yielding an overall prevalence of 6.16%, with significant differences in HPV16 detection rates between 2022 and 2025. The HPV16 lineage A prototype was the most prevalent and was associated with increased risk of persistent infection and cervical disease, including cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and cervical cancer. Viral load differed significantly between disease and lesion-free groups, as well as between persistent infection and viral clearance groups, but no significant association was observed between viral load and E6/E7 mutations (E6 L83V or E7 N29S).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the HPV16 lineage A prototype plays a key role in viral pathogenicity and persistence, and that viral load correlates positively with disease severity and persistence, suggesting its potential utility as a clinical biomarker.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform HPV16 surveillance strategies by highlighting the importance of lineage A in persistent infection and disease progression, and could support the use of viral load as a biomarker for risk stratification in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single geographic region and the need for further research to clarify additional molecular mechanisms underlying HPV16 evolution and adaptation, warranting a moderate strength of evidence.

血清流行率 医护人员 HPV 感染的 血清流行率 分子确认

Seroprevalence of HPV Infection in Healthcare Workers with Molecular Confirmation of High-Risk HPV
作者： Ahmed Qasim Risan, Ahmed Hasan Mohammed
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在确定抗 HPV IgG 和 IgM 抗体的 血清流行率,并检测医护人员中循环的 HR-HPV 脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA),以评估潜在的职业癌症风险。材料和方法 一项横断面研究评估了伊拉克济加尔省 Al-Shatra 总医院和 Al-Haboubi 教学医院综合医院的 170 名医护人员。结果观察到 血清流行率 非常高,分别有 95.9% 和 90.0% 的参与者抗 HPV IgG 和 IgM 检测呈阳性。结论 广泛存在的血清学 患病率 与低分子检测率之间的显着差异可能表明职业接触 HR-HPV 与短暂性病毒血症之间存在潜在关联。这些发现凸显了医护人员迫切需要采取严格的个人防护措施、改善手术过程中的通风以及有针对性的预防性 疫苗接种 策略,以降低 HPV 引起的癌症的风险。本研究旨在确定伊拉克济加尔省 Al-Shatra 总医院和 Al-Haboubi 教学医院医护人员中抗 HPV IgG 和 IgM 抗体的 血清流行率,并使用常规聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 检测作为短暂性 DNA 血症的指标来调查血清中循环 高危型HPV DNA 片段(HPV-16 和 HPV-18)的存在。摘要 简介 高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因型,特别是 HPV-16 和 HPV-18,是各种癌症的主要病因。医护人员通过吸入医疗手术过程中产生的手术烟雾和气溶胶,面临着非性职业暴露于致癌 HPV 的重大风险。血清浓度...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了致癌性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的全球高负担,特别是 HPV-16 和 HPV-18,它们占 宫颈癌 病例的 70% 以上。其目的是确定抗 HPV 抗体的 血清流行率 并检测医护人员(潜在职业接触人群)中循环的 血清流行率 DNA。
设计与对象
对伊拉克济加尔省 Al-Shatra 综合医院和 Al-Haboubi 教学医院的医护人员进行了一项横断面研究。参与者是既往没有感染 HPV 疫苗接种、没有性传播感染症状的成年人,并提供了知情同意书。排除对象包括患有慢性全身性疾病的个体、孕妇和 60 岁或以上的成年人。
核心方法
使用商业 ELISA 试剂盒对血清抗 HPV IgG 和 IgM 抗体进行定量。对 100 个血清阳性样本(IgM > 0.25 nmol/L)的子集进行常规 PCR 检测 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 的循环游离 DNA。由于探索性设计和 PCR 阳性病例有限,统计分析使用非参数检验。
主要结果
血清流行率 非常高:95.9% (163/170) 的抗 HPV IgG 检测呈阳性,90.0% (153/170) 的抗 HPV IgM 检测呈阳性。分子分析仅在 7.0% (7/100) 的子集中检测到 HPV-16 DNA,而未检测到 HPV-18。PCR 阳性个体的 IgM 水平显着高于 PCR 阴性个体 (p < 0.0001)。
解释与创新
作者将广泛的血清阳性和低分子检测之间的差异解释为表明短暂的病毒血症与医护人员的职业暴露有关。他们强调这是职业 队列研究 中的一个新颖观察。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,单独的血清学筛查可能会高估医护人员的活动性 HPV 感染风险,这强调了 监测 和感染控制计划中分子确认的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用了便利抽样并排除了高风险群体,限制了普遍性。疫苗接种 状态是自我报告的,引入了潜在的回忆偏差。PCR 阳性病例数量较少,限制了先进的统计模型,导致证据强度中等。

Seroprevalence of HPV Infection in Healthcare Workers with Molecular Confirmation of High-Risk HPV

Authors: Ahmed Qasim Risan, Ahmed Hasan Mohammed
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to determine the seroprevalence of anti-HPV IgG and IgM antibodies and detect circulating HR-HPV deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA) among healthcare workers to assess potential occupational cancer risks. Materials and Methods A cross-sectional study evaluated 170 healthcare workers from general hospitals in Al-Shatra General Hospital and Al-Haboubi Teaching Hospital in Dhi Qar Governorate, Iraq. Results A remarkably high seroprevalence was observed, with 95.9% and 90.0% of participants testing positive for anti-HPV IgG and IgM, respectively. Conclusion The substantial discrepancy between the widespread serological prevalence and the low rate of molecular detection may indicate a potential association between occupational exposure to HR-HPV and transient viremia. These findings highlight the urgent need for stringent personal protective measures, improved ventilation during procedures, and targeted prophylactic vaccination strategies for healthcare personnel to mitigate the risk of HPV-driven cancers. This study aimed to determine the seroprevalence of anti-HPV IgG and IgM antibodies among healthcare workers in Al-Shatra General Hospital and Al-Haboubi Teaching Hospital in Dhi Qar Governorate, Iraq and to investigate the presence of circulating high-risk HPV DNA fragments (HPV-16 and HPV-18) in serum using conventional polymerase chain reaction (PCR) assays as an indicator of transient DNAemia. Abstract Introduction High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes, particularly HPV-16 and HPV-18, are the primary etiologic agents of various carcinomas. Healthcare workers face a significant risk of non-sexual occupational exposure to oncogenic HPV through the inhalation of surgical smoke and aerosols generated during medical procedures.
