宫颈阴道微生物组:HPV 感染和疾病的新决定因素

The cervicovaginal microbiome: an emerging determinant of HPV infection and disease
作者： Megan E. Spurgeon
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一种亲上皮病毒,可引起从皮肤疣到侵袭性癌症等多种疾病。HPV 对皮肤、口腔和肛门生殖道的上皮衬里表现出趋向性。HPV 的一个子集(称为 高危型HPV)具有致癌潜力,导致全球近三分之一的病毒相关癌症,包括基本上所有 宫颈癌、大多数其他肛门生殖器癌症以及越来越多的头颈癌。大量且不断增长的临床和流行病学研究已将 宿主 微生物组(即体内和体外的微生物集合)确定为 HPV 感染、持续存在和疾病发展的关键决定因素。在这篇综述中,概述了目前对 宿主 宫颈阴道微生物组 (CVM) 细菌成分与 HPV 发病机制 各方面之间关联的理解。还介绍了 CVM 影响 HPV 感染和肿瘤疾病发展的潜在机制。研究 HPV 和微生物组的固有挑战不仅阻碍了定义 HPV-CVM 相互作用的潜在机制的进展,而且也阻碍了解决诸如因果关系、时间动态和这种关系的方向性等剩余问题的能力。新兴的体外和体内模型为解决这些突出问题提供了强大的机会,并增加我们对导致 HPV 感染和随后疾病的跨界相互作用的理解。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、其亲上皮性质及其在引起皮肤疣、肛门生殖器癌和头颈癌等疾病中的作用,重点关注宫颈阴道微生物组 (CVM) 作为 HPV 感染、持续性和疾病发展的决定因素。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了 宿主 微生物组(特别是 CVM 的细菌成分)的临床和流行病学研究,并讨论了 CVM 影响 HPV 发病机制 的潜在机制。它强调了研究 HPV-微生物组相互作用的挑战以及对先进模型来解决因果关系、时间动态和方向性的需求。
共识与结论
高危型HPV,包括 HPV16、HPV18、HPV31、HPV33、HPV45、HPV52 和 HPV58,被确定为致癌因子,导致全球近三分之一的病毒相关癌症,包括基本上所有 宫颈癌 以及相当比例的肛门生殖器癌和头颈癌。
争议与缺口
研究 HPV 和微生物组的固有挑战阻碍了在定义 HPV-CVM 相互作用的潜在机制以及解决这种关系的因果关系、时间动态和方向性方面取得进展。该评论指出,尽管出现了体外和体内模型,但这些差距仍然存在。
科研与实践启示
新兴的体外和体内模型被认为是解决有关 HPV-CVM 相互作用的突出问题(包括因果关系和时间动态)的关键工具,从而增进对导致 HPV 感染和疾病的跨界相互作用的理解。

The cervicovaginal microbiome: an emerging determinant of HPV infection and disease

Authors: Megan E. Spurgeon
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) are epitheliotropic viruses that cause diseases ranging from cutaneous warts to invasive cancers. HPVs exhibit tropism for the epithelial lining of the skin, oral cavity, and anogenital tract. A subset of HPVs, known as high-risk HPVs, have oncogenic potential and cause nearly one-third of all virus-associated cancers worldwide, including essentially all cervical cancers, most other anogenital cancers, and a growing percentage of head and neck cancers. A large and growing body of clinical and epidemiological research has established the host microbiome, or the collection of microorganisms on and within the body, as a key determinant of HPV infection, persistence, and disease development. In this review, an overview of the current understanding of associations between bacterial components of the host cervicovaginal microbiome (CVM) and aspects of HPV pathogenesis is discussed. Potential mechanisms through which the CVM influences HPV infection and neoplastic disease development are also presented. The inherent challenges of studying HPVs and the microbiome have impeded progress to define not only the underlying mechanisms involved in HPV-CVM interactions, but also the ability to address remaining questions such as causality, temporal dynamics, and the directionality of this relationship. Emerging in vitro and in vivo models provide powerful opportunities to address these outstanding questions and increase our understanding of trans-kingdom interactions that contribute to HPV infection and subsequent disease.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomaviruses (HPVs), their epitheliotropic nature, and their role in causing diseases such as cutaneous warts, anogenital cancers, and head and neck cancers, focusing on the cervicovaginal microbiome (CVM) as a determinant of HPV infection, persistence, and disease development.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes clinical and epidemiological research on the host microbiome, specifically the bacterial components of the CVM, and discusses potential mechanisms by which the CVM influences HPV pathogenesis. It highlights the challenges in studying HPV-microbiome interactions and the need for advanced models to address causality, temporal dynamics, and directionality.
Consensus and conclusions
High-risk HPVs, including HPV16, HPV18, HPV31, HPV33, HPV45, HPV52, and HPV58, are established as oncogenic agents responsible for nearly one-third of virus-associated cancers globally, including essentially all cervical cancers and a significant proportion of anogenital and head and neck cancers.
Controversies and gaps
The inherent challenges in studying HPVs and the microbiome impede progress in defining the underlying mechanisms of HPV-CVM interactions, as well as addressing causality, temporal dynamics, and the directionality of this relationship. The review notes that these gaps persist despite emerging in vitro and in vivo models.
Research and practice implications
Emerging in vitro and in vivo models are identified as critical tools to address outstanding questions about HPV-CVM interactions, including causality and temporal dynamics, thereby advancing understanding of trans-kingdom interactions that contribute to HPV infection and disease.

HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌:AJCC 第 9 版 TNM 分期的影像框架

HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Imaging Framework for AJCC Version 9 TNM Staging
作者： Pranjal Rai, Adam L. Holtzman, Alok A. Bhatt
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC),重点关注影像学在 AJCC 第 9 版 TNM 分期系统中的作用,特别评估 HPV 阳性疾病中的 T、N 和 M 类别以及结外扩散 (iENE)。
证据基础与方法
该文章是一篇叙述性综述,概述了对 HPV 相关 OPSCC 进行分期的放射学方法,强调使用 CT、MRI 和 PET 评估肿瘤范围、淋巴结疾病、iENE 和远处转移,并讨论了指导 治疗 选择的多学科因素。
共识与结论
审查证实,AJCC 第 9 版将 iENE 纳入临床淋巴结分期,将病理结外扩展纳入 HPV 相关 OPSCC 的病理淋巴结分期,同时保留第八版中的 T 和 M 类别定义。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了解读 iENE 时读者间的变异性和不完善的诊断性能,并指出需要高级证据来确定其作为临床 N 类别修饰符的一致可重复性、增量预后价值。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要进一步的高级证据来验证 iENE 的预后效用,并呼吁在临床实践中谨慎解释,同时还强调放射科医生在多学科团队中对于准确分期和 治疗 规划的核心作用。

HPV-associated Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma: An Imaging Framework for AJCC Version 9 TNM Staging

Authors: Pranjal Rai, Adam L. Holtzman, Alok A. Bhatt
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is biologically and prognostically distinct from HPV-negative disease, and staging directly impacts treatment. The American Joint Committee on Cancer (AJCC) and Union for International Cancer Control version 9 TNM staging system incorporates unequivocal imaging-detected and/or clinically detected extranodal extension (iENE) into clinical nodal staging and definitive pathologic extranodal extension into pathologic nodal staging. Additionally, it provides more explicit anatomic definitions for advanced local disease (clinical T4) while maintaining T and M category definitions from the eighth edition. Radiologists play a central role in the multidisciplinary team by evaluating endophytic tumor extent, regional nodal disease and iENE, and distant metastasis using CT, MRI, and PET. This article reviews clinically relevant oropharyngeal anatomy and proposes a practical radiologic approach to assigning T, N, and M categories in HPV-associated OPSCC. It emphasizes cautious interpretation of iENE because of interreader variability and imperfect diagnostic performance, with further high-grade evidence needed to establish its consistently reproducible, incremental prognostic value as a clinical N category modifier. It also highlights multidisciplinary factors that guide selection of surgical resection versus nonsurgical therapy. Posttreatment evaluation is discussed in the context of blood-based biomarkers such as tumor tissue-modified viral HPV DNA. The goal of this article is to provide a practical, staging-focused imaging framework for evaluation of HPV-associated OPSCC. Keywords: CT, MR Imaging, Head/Neck, Neck, Pharynx, Surgery, Human Papillomavirus, Squamous Cell Carcinoma, Oropharynx, Transoral Robotic Surgery, Oropharyngeal Carcinoma, TTMV HPV DNA © RSNA, 2026.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), focusing on the role of imaging in the AJCC version 9 TNM staging system, specifically evaluating T, N, and M categories and extranodal extension (iENE) in HPV-positive disease.
Evidence base and method
The article is a narrative review that outlines a radiologic approach to staging HPV-associated OPSCC, emphasizing the evaluation of tumor extent, nodal disease, iENE, and distant metastasis using CT, MRI, and PET, and discusses multidisciplinary factors guiding treatment selection.
Consensus and conclusions
The review confirms that AJCC version 9 incorporates iENE into clinical nodal staging and pathologic extranodal extension into pathologic nodal staging for HPV-associated OPSCC, while maintaining T and M category definitions from the eighth edition.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights interreader variability and imperfect diagnostic performance in interpreting iENE, noting that high-grade evidence is needed to establish its consistently reproducible, incremental prognostic value as a clinical N category modifier.
Research and practice implications
The review underscores the need for further high-grade evidence to validate the prognostic utility of iENE and calls for cautious interpretation in clinical practice, while also emphasizing the central role of radiologists in multidisciplinary teams for accurate staging and treatment planning.

人类舌癌人群中预后指标、HPV 状态和癌症干细胞标记物之间的相关性。

Correlation Between Prognostic Indicators, HPV Status, and Cancer Stem Cell Markers in a Population of Human Tongue Carcinomas.
作者： Adriely Primo da Silva, Beatriz Gomes Carreira Sartori, Carolina de Barros Gigliotti Vendramini, Adauto José Ferreira Nunes, Maria Renata Sales Nogueira
摘要中文翻译背景:口腔鳞状细胞癌(OSCC)是一个重要的公共卫生问题。虽然烟草和酒精是既定的危险因素,但 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在口腔癌发生中的作用仍存在争议。癌症干细胞(CSC)与肿瘤侵袭性和治疗耐药性有关。本研究调查了 HPV 感染、CSC 标志物表达(CD44、CD98、ALDH1)和舌 SCC 临床病理参数之间的相关性。方法:对 2013-2018 年 1047 例 OSCC 病例进行回顾性 队列研究 分析。从452例舌SCC中随机抽取83例福尔马林固定石蜡包埋标本。使用 qPCR 和通用引物和类型特异性引物(HPV 6、11、16、18)进行 HPV DNA 检测。使用数字图像分析(IHC Profiler)评估和定量 CD44、CD98 和 ALDH1 的免疫组织化学表达。统计分析包括 Kaplan-Meier 生存曲线、Kruskal-Wallis、卡方和 Spearman 相关检验。结果:2/83 例 (2.4%) 中检测到 HPV DNA:1 例 HPV16 阳性,1 例合并感染 (HPV6/18)。CD44 表达普遍较高,-High 和 -Positive 类别之间没有显着差异 (p > 0.9999),但两者均显着高于 -Low 和 -Negative 得分 (p < 0.0001)。ALDH1 表现出局灶性染色模式。ALDH1-高分明显低于-阳性和-低分(p < 0.01),并且与晚期临床分期(Spearman r:0.5062;p = 0.0007)和较高的肿瘤分级(Spearman r:0.3352;p = 0.0322)呈显着正相关。未检测到 CD98。HPV 与临床分期之间观察到负相关(Spearman r:-0.2377;p = 0.0305);然而,由于 HPV 阳性病例数量较少,必须谨慎解释这一发现。结论:我们的研究结果表明,队列研究 中存在独特的 CSC 表达谱,其特征是普遍存在的 CD44 表达和罕见但高度侵袭性的 ALDH1-High 亚群,同时伴有低 HPV 检测。ALDH1-High 评分与晚期分期和分级的强正相关性凸显了其作为肿瘤侵袭的关键驱动因素的潜在作用,值得作为预后标志物进行进一步研究。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究旨在探讨舌鳞状细胞癌(SCC)中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染、癌症干细胞(CSC)标志物表达(CD44、CD98、ALDH1)和临床病理参数之间的相关性。重点是 HPV 类型和明确的 HPV 相关疾病,特别强调 HPV 在口腔癌发生中的作用及其与 CSC 标志物的关系。
证据基础与方法
该研究是对 2013-2018 年 1047 例 OSCC 病例进行回顾性 队列研究 分析,随机选择 83 例福尔马林固定石蜡包埋标本。使用 qPCR 和通用引物和类型特异性引物(HPV 6、11、16、18)进行 HPV DNA 检测。使用数字图像分析(IHC Profiler)评估 CD44、CD98 和 ALDH1 的免疫组织化学表达。统计分析包括 Kaplan-Meier 生存曲线、Kruskal-Wallis、卡方和 Spearman 相关检验。
共识与结论
仅 2/83 例(2.4%)检测到 HPV DNA,其中 1 例为 HPV16 阳性,另一例显示同时感染 HPV6/18。CD44表达普遍较高,高表达和阳性类别之间没有显着差异。ALDH1-高分出现的频率明显较低,并且与晚期临床分期和较高的肿瘤分级相关。未检测到 CD98。该研究表明舌 SCC 中存在独特的 CSC 表达谱,其中 ALDH1-High 是肿瘤侵袭的潜在标志物。
争议与缺口
研究指出,由于 HPV 阳性病例数量较少,必须谨慎解释 HPV 与临床分期之间的负相关性(Spearman r:-0.2377;p = 0.0305)。此外,HPV 在口腔癌发生中的作用仍存在争议,该研究没有提供足够的证据来明确解决这一争议。
科研与实践启示
ALDH1-High 分数与晚期阶段和等级之间的强正相关性突出表明需要进一步研究 ALDH1 作为预后标志物。舌鳞状细胞癌中 HPV 的检出率较低,表明 HPV 可能不是这种特定癌症类型的主要驱动因素,但需要更多的研究来证实这一点,并探索所观察到的相关性背后的机制。

Correlation Between Prognostic Indicators, HPV Status, and Cancer Stem Cell Markers in a Population of Human Tongue Carcinomas.

Authors: Adriely Primo da Silva, Beatriz Gomes Carreira Sartori, Carolina de Barros Gigliotti Vendramini, Adauto José Ferreira Nunes, Maria Renata Sales Nogueira
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Oral squamous cell carcinoma (OSCC) is a significant public health issue. While tobacco and alcohol are established risk factors, the role of human papillomavirus (HPV) in oral carcinogenesis remains debated. Cancer stem cells (CSCs) are implicated in tumor aggressiveness and therapy resistance. This study investigated the correlation between HPV infection, CSC marker expression (CD44, CD98, ALDH1), and clinicopathological parameters in tongue SCCs. METHODS: A retrospective cohort of 1047 OSCC cases (2013-2018) was reviewed. From 452 tongue SCCs, 83 formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded specimens were randomly selected. HPV DNA detection was performed using qPCR with generic and type-specific primers (HPV 6, 11, 16, 18). Immunohistochemical expression of CD44, CD98, and ALDH1 was evaluated and quantified using digital image analysis (IHC Profiler). Statistical analyses included Kaplan-Meier survival curves, Kruskal-Wallis, Chi-square, and Spearman correlation tests. RESULTS: HPV DNA was detected in 2/83 cases (2.4%): one HPV16-positive and one with co-infection (HPV6/18). CD44 expression was ubiquitously high, with no significant difference between -High and -Positive categories (p > 0.9999), but both were significantly higher than -Low and -Negative scores (p < 0.0001). ALDH1 exhibited a focal staining pattern. The ALDH1-High score was significantly less frequent than -Positive and -Low scores (p < 0.01) and showed a significant positive correlation with both advanced clinical stage (Spearman r: 0.5062; p = 0.0007) and higher tumor grade (Spearman r: 0.3352; p = 0.0322). CD98 was not detected. An inverse correlation was observed between HPV and clinical staging (Spearman r: -0.2377; p = 0.0305); however, due to the low number of HPV-positive cases, this finding must be interpreted with caution. CONCLUSION: Our findings suggest a distinct CSC expression profile in this cohort, characterized by ubiquitous CD44 expression and a rare but highly aggressive ALDH1-High subpopulation, concomitant with low HPV detection. The strong positive correlation of ALDH1-High score with advanced stage and grade highlights its potential role as a key driver of tumor aggression, warranting further investigation as a prognostic marker.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to investigate the correlation between human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, cancer stem cell (CSC) marker expression (CD44, CD98, ALDH1), and clinicopathological parameters in tongue squamous cell carcinomas (SCCs). The focus was on HPV types and explicitly HPV-associated diseases, with a particular emphasis on the role of HPV in oral carcinogenesis and its relationship with CSC markers.
Evidence base and method
The study was a retrospective cohort analysis of 1047 OSCC cases (2013-2018), with 83 formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded specimens randomly selected. HPV DNA detection was conducted using qPCR with generic and type-specific primers (HPV 6, 11, 16, 18). Immunohistochemical expression of CD44, CD98, and ALDH1 was evaluated using digital image analysis (IHC Profiler). Statistical analyses included Kaplan-Meier survival curves, Kruskal-Wallis, Chi-square, and Spearman correlation tests.
Consensus and conclusions
HPV DNA was detected in only 2/83 cases (2.4%), with one being HPV16-positive and another showing co-infection with HPV6/18. CD44 expression was universally high, with no significant difference between high and positive categories. ALDH1-High scores were significantly less frequent and correlated with advanced clinical stage and higher tumor grade. CD98 was not detected. The study suggests a distinct CSC expression profile in tongue SCCs, with ALDH1-High being a potential marker for tumor aggression.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that the inverse correlation between HPV and clinical staging (Spearman r: -0.2377; p = 0.0305) must be interpreted cautiously due to the low number of HPV-positive cases. Additionally, the role of HPV in oral carcinogenesis remains debated, and the study does not provide sufficient evidence to resolve this controversy definitively.
Research and practice implications
The strong positive correlation between ALDH1-High scores and advanced stage and grade highlights the need for further investigation into ALDH1 as a prognostic marker. The low detection rate of HPV in tongue SCCs suggests that HPV may not be a major driver of this specific cancer type, but more research is needed to confirm this and to explore the mechanisms underlying the observed correlations.

中国安徽省医学生 HPV 疫苗 知晓率、使用率、意向及影响因素:一项横断面研究

HPV vaccine awareness, uptake, intention, and influencing factors among medical students in Anhui, China: A cross-sectional study
作者： Qian Huang, Jian‐Hui Xiong, Zihsyuan Liu, Yan-Yu Li, Xue-Li Wang, Meng‐Han Wang, Hai-Feng Pan, Sha-Sha Tao
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究的重点是评估中国安徽两性医学生对 HPV 疫苗 的认知、接受度和潜在障碍,重点是了解影响 疫苗接种 率的因素,尤其是男性。
证据基础与方法
该研究是2025年10月至2025年12月进行的一项横断面在线调查,涉及安徽医科大学831名全日制本科生。审查方法包括多变量分析,以确定社会经济因素与 疫苗接种 意图之间的关联。
共识与结论
研究发现,虽然HPV 疫苗知晓率较高(88.33%),但实际疫苗接种知晓率较低,为25.55%,其中女性知晓率(54.40%)高于男性。多变量分析表明,女性 疫苗接种 的吸收与家庭社会经济因素显着相关,特别是每月生活津贴和母亲受教育程度。在男学生中,对国家男性 HPV 疫苗接种 政策和可接受的 疫苗 成本的态度与 疫苗接种 意图相关。
争议与缺口
该研究强调了 疫苗接种 意图的性别差异,女性的意图明显高于男性。然而,证据并未全面了解这种差异背后的原因或解决这一问题的潜在干预措施的有效性。
科研与实践启示
为了提高中国医科大学生中 HPV 疫苗 的感染率,需要有针对性的教育策略和更容易获得的 疫苗接种 支持,特别是对于男学生和社会经济资源较少的学生。

HPV vaccine awareness, uptake, intention, and influencing factors among medical students in Anhui, China: A cross-sectional study

Authors: Qian Huang, Jian‐Hui Xiong, Zihsyuan Liu, Yan-Yu Li, Xue-Li Wang, Meng‐Han Wang, Hai-Feng Pan, Sha-Sha Tao
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Although prophylactic vaccination is highly effective against human papillomavirus (HPV), HPV vaccine uptake among Chinese college students remains insufficient, and evidence among medical university students, particularly males, remains limited. Following the recent approval of male HPV vaccines in China, this study evaluated vaccine awareness, acceptance, and potential barriers among both sexes. A cross-sectional online survey of 831 full-time undergraduate students at Anhui Medical University was conducted from October to December 2025. While overall HPV vaccine awareness was high (88.33%), the actual vaccination rate remained low at 25.55% (54.40% among females). Overall vaccination intention was 67.27%, exhibiting a profound gender disparity (92.80% in females vs. 46.27% in males, P < .001). Multivariable analyses showed that female vaccination uptake was significantly associated with familial socioeconomic factors, particularly monthly living allowance and maternal educational attainment. In male students, neutral or opposing attitudes toward a national male HPV vaccination policy were associated with lower vaccination intention (OR = 0.61, 95% CI: 0.409–0.898; OR = 0.27, 95% CI: 0.084–0.756), whereas a maximum acceptable vaccine cost of RMB 1,000–2,000 was associated with higher intention (OR = 1.61, 95% CI: 1.003–2.592). Additionally, female students showed higher HPV-related knowledge scores and greater reliance on interpersonal and professional information sources. Ultimately, these findings suggest that improving HPV vaccine uptake among medical university students in China may require targeted educational strategies and more accessible vaccination support, particularly for male students and those with fewer socioeconomic resources.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on evaluating HPV vaccine awareness, acceptance, and potential barriers among both sexes of medical students in Anhui, China, with an emphasis on understanding factors influencing vaccination rates, particularly among males.
Evidence base and method
The study is a cross-sectional online survey conducted from October to December 2025, involving 831 full-time undergraduate students at Anhui Medical University. The review method includes multivariable analyses to identify associations between socioeconomic factors and vaccination intention.
Consensus and conclusions
The study found that while HPV vaccine awareness was high (88.33%), the actual vaccination rate was low at 25.55%, with females having a higher rate (54.40%) than males. Multivariable analyses indicated that female vaccination uptake was significantly associated with familial socioeconomic factors, particularly monthly living allowance and maternal educational attainment. In male students, attitudes toward the national male HPV vaccination policy and acceptable vaccine cost were linked to vaccination intention.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlights a gender disparity in vaccination intention, with females showing significantly higher intention than males. However, the evidence does not provide a comprehensive understanding of the reasons behind this disparity or the effectiveness of potential interventions to address it.
Research and practice implications
To improve HPV vaccine uptake among medical university students in China, targeted educational strategies and more accessible vaccination support are needed, especially for male students and those with fewer socioeconomic resources.

处于衰老和癌症十字路口的肿瘤病毒:分子机制和关键信号通路。

Oncoviruses at the Crossroads of Ageing and Cancer: Molecular Mechanisms and Key Signalling Pathways.
作者： Noor Allehyani, Mohammed Alissa, Abdullah Alghamdi, Mohammed A Alshehri, Ghadah S Abusalim, Tawfiq N Juraybi, Meshari A Alsuwat, Awaji Y Safhi
摘要中文翻译肿瘤病毒是导致大部分人类癌症的原因,并且已成为 宿主 细胞途径的关键调节剂,该途径也控制着衰老。病毒肿瘤发生、衰老和癌症的融合反映了共同的分子机制,包括基因组不稳定性、表观遗传改变、端粒磨损和慢性炎症。越来越多的证据表明,人乳头瘤病毒、Epstein-Barr病毒、乙型和丙型肝炎病毒以及卡波西肉瘤相关疱疹病毒等肿瘤病毒会主动重编程宿主信号网络,以促进持续感染、免疫逃避和恶性转化。这篇综述综合了目前关于肿瘤病毒如何与衰老和癌症的关键特征相交叉的知识,特别强调了桥接这些过程的信号通路。包括 p53、mTOR、NF-κB、PI3K/AKT 和 Wnt/β-catenin 在内的中央通路经常成为病毒蛋白的目标,导致细胞存活、增殖和衰老失调。这些相互作用不仅促进肿瘤发生,而且加速与衰老相关的细胞功能障碍,例如免疫衰老和炎症。此外,我们讨论了病毒诱导的肿瘤微环境调节和 宿主 免疫应答 如何促进衰老与癌症之间的动态相互作用。了解这些相互关联的途径可以为了解肿瘤病毒作为与年龄相关的衰退和癌症发展的驱动因素的双重作用提供有价值的见解。阐明这些机制可能会揭示预防和治疗老龄化人群中与病毒相关的恶性肿瘤的新治疗靶点和策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇综述探讨了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与衰老和癌症关键标志相交叉的分子机制,重点关注桥接这些过程的信号通路。核心问题是 HPV 如何调节 宿主 细胞通路以促进持续感染、免疫逃避和恶性转化,重点是其在与年龄相关的细胞功能障碍中的作用。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 HPV 在衰老和癌症中作用的当前知识的叙述性综合。它不报告特定的数据库、搜索日期或荟萃分析方法。这些证据源自对 HPV 及其分子相互作用的现有文献的一般科学理解和解释。
共识与结论
人们一致认为,包括 HPV 在内的肿瘤病毒会主动重编程 宿主 信号网络,以促进持续感染、免疫逃避和恶性转化。p53、mTOR、NF-κB、PI3K/AKT 和 Wnt/β-连环蛋白等中央通路经常成为病毒蛋白的目标,导致细胞存活、增殖和衰老失调。这些相互作用导致肿瘤发生和衰老相关的细胞功能障碍。
争议与缺口
该综述强调,病毒肿瘤发生、衰老和癌症的趋同反映了共同的分子机制,但证据并未报告具体的争议或相互矛盾的发现。对于病毒诱导的肿瘤微环境调节的精确机制和宿主 免疫应答缺乏详细的讨论。
科研与实践启示
了解这些相互关联的途径可能会揭示预防和治疗老龄化人群中与病毒相关的恶性肿瘤的新治疗靶点和策略。该综述表明,需要进一步研究来探索病毒诱导的肿瘤微环境调节与 宿主 免疫应答 之间的动态相互作用。

Oncoviruses at the Crossroads of Ageing and Cancer: Molecular Mechanisms and Key Signalling Pathways.

Authors: Noor Allehyani, Mohammed Alissa, Abdullah Alghamdi, Mohammed A Alshehri, Ghadah S Abusalim, Tawfiq N Juraybi, Meshari A Alsuwat, Awaji Y Safhi
Original abstract (English)
Oncoviruses are responsible for a substantial fraction of human cancers and have emerged as critical modulators of host cellular pathways that also govern ageing. The convergence of viral oncogenesis, ageing, and cancer reflects shared molecular mechanisms, including genomic instability, epigenetic alterations, telomere attrition, and chronic inflammation. Increasing evidence indicates that oncoviruses such as human papillomavirus, Epstein-Barr virus, hepatitis B and C viruses, and Kaposi's sarcoma-associated herpesvirus actively reprogramme host signalling networks to promote persistent infection, immune evasion, and malignant transformation. This review synthesises current knowledge on how oncoviruses intersect with key hallmarks of ageing and cancer, with particular emphasis on the signalling pathways that bridge these processes. Central pathways, including p53, mTOR, NF-κB, PI3K/AKT, and Wnt/β-catenin, are frequently targeted by viral proteins, leading to dysregulated cell survival, proliferation, and senescence. These interactions not only facilitate tumourigenesis but also accelerate ageing-associated cellular dysfunctions, such as immunosenescence and inflammaging. Furthermore, we discuss how virus-induced modulation of the tumour microenvironment and host immune responses contributes to the dynamic interplay between ageing and cancer. Understanding these interconnected pathways provides valuable insights into the dual role of oncoviruses as drivers of both age-related decline and cancer development. Elucidating these mechanisms may reveal novel therapeutic targets and strategies for preventing and treating virus-associated malignancies in ageing populations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review explores the molecular mechanisms by which human papillomaviruses (HPV) intersect with key hallmarks of ageing and cancer, focusing on the signalling pathways that bridge these processes. The central question is how HPV modulates host cellular pathways to promote persistent infection, immune evasion, and malignant transformation, with an emphasis on its role in age-related cellular dysfunctions.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of current knowledge on HPV's role in ageing and cancer. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods. The evidence is derived from general scientific understanding and interpretation of existing literature on HPV and its molecular interactions.
Consensus and conclusions
There is consensus that oncoviruses, including HPV, actively reprogramme host signalling networks to promote persistent infection, immune evasion, and malignant transformation. Central pathways such as p53, mTOR, NF-κB, PI3K/AKT, and Wnt/β-catenin are frequently targeted by viral proteins, leading to dysregulated cell survival, proliferation, and senescence. These interactions contribute to both tumourigenesis and ageing-associated cellular dysfunctions.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that the convergence of viral oncogenesis, ageing, and cancer reflects shared molecular mechanisms, but the evidence does not report specific controversies or conflicting findings. There is a lack of detailed discussion on the precise mechanisms of virus-induced modulation of the tumour microenvironment and host immune responses.
Research and practice implications
Understanding these interconnected pathways may reveal novel therapeutic targets and strategies for preventing and treating virus-associated malignancies in ageing populations. The review suggests that further research is needed to explore the dynamic interplay between virus-induced modulation of the tumour microenvironment and host immune responses.

男性生殖器硬化性苔藓

Male genital lichen sclerosus
作者： Georgios Kravvas, Rory Barry, Richard E Watchorn, Christopher B. Bunker
摘要中文翻译男性生殖器硬化性苔藓(MGLSc)是一种慢性炎症性皮肤病,可能导致包茎、尿道疾病、性功能障碍和阴茎鳞状细胞癌(PeSCC)。尽管其病因尚不完全清楚,但临床和流行病学观察越来越多地支持易感上皮长期闭塞暴露于尿液的核心作用。病变在闭塞、尿液暴露部位的分布、包皮环切术后的高缓解率、新生儿割包皮人群中疾病的罕见性、与微失禁和泌尿外科手术的相关性以及尿道造口部位病变的发生都表明刺激性 发病机制。继发性炎症、纤维化和上皮修复过程似乎出现在这种主要损伤的下游。临床上,MGLSc 表现为苍白、红斑、裂痕和进行性疤痕,常伴有细微的尿道异常,导致排尿后滴尿。长期患病可能导致尿道口狭窄、尿道狭窄和功能障碍。MGLSc、分化的阴茎上皮内瘤变和 PeSCC 之间存在公认的关联,代表一种通常独立于 人乳头瘤病毒 感染和紫外线辐射的致癌途径。治疗的目的是控制炎症、恢复上皮屏障并减少闭塞。有效的局部皮质类固醇仍然是一线治疗,而当局部治疗失败时,包皮环切术非常有效。对于难治性或复杂的疾病,可能需要额外的医疗和手术 治疗。本综述总结了目前关于 MGLSc 的临床特征、病理生理学、恶性潜力和治疗的知识,重点是与闭塞和尿液暴露相关的机制。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该范围检查男性生殖器硬化性苔藓 (MGLSc),这是一种阴茎慢性炎症性皮肤病,重点关注其临床表现、进展为阴茎鳞状细胞癌 (PeSCC) 的可能性,以及其 发病机制 和治疗的核心问题。该疾病的特点是慢性炎症、疤痕和功能障碍,其病因学和致癌潜力存在争议。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了 MGLSc 的当前知识,强调了与闭塞和尿液暴露相关的机制。它整合了临床观察、流行病学模式和管理策略,无需报告系统搜索方法、资格标准或研究计数。
共识与结论
越来越多的证据表明,易感上皮细胞长期闭塞地暴露于尿液是 MGLSc 的主要病因。继发性炎症、纤维化和上皮修复过程出现在这种损伤的下游。临床上,MGLSc 表现为苍白、红斑、裂痕和疤痕,通常伴有轻微的尿道异常。长期患病可能导致尿道口狭窄、尿道狭窄和功能障碍。
争议与缺口
MGLSc 的病因学仍不完全清楚,关于慢性尿液暴露与其他潜在触发因素的相对贡献的问题尚未解决。该综述强调了在理解慢性炎症与恶性转化之间的精确机制方面的差距,特别是独立于 人乳头瘤病毒 感染和紫外线辐射的致癌途径。
科研与实践启示
研究重点包括阐明慢性尿液暴露与炎症和纤维化之间的分子和细胞途径,并阐明从 MGLSc 发展为 PeSCC 的潜在机制。临床上应重视早期诊断和干预,预防尿道口狭窄、尿道狭窄等并发症。

Male genital lichen sclerosus

Authors: Georgios Kravvas, Rory Barry, Richard E Watchorn, Christopher B. Bunker
Original abstract (English)
Male genital lichen sclerosus (MGLSc) is a chronic inflammatory dermatosis that may lead to phimosis, urethral disease, sexual dysfunction and penile squamous cell carcinoma (PeSCC). Although its aetiology remains incompletely understood, clinical and epidemiological observations increasingly support a central role for chronic occluded exposure of susceptible epithelium to urine. The distribution of lesions to occluded, urine-exposed sites, the high rate of remission after circumcision, the rarity of disease in neonatally circumcised populations, the association with microincontinence and urological procedures, and the occurrence of lesions at urinary stoma sites all suggest an irritative pathogenesis. Secondary inflammatory, fibrotic and epithelial repair processes appear to arise downstream of this primary insult. Clinically, MGLSc presents with pallor, erythema, fissuring and progressive scarring, often accompanied by subtle urethral abnormalities contributing to post-micturition dribbling. Long-standing disease may result in meatal stenosis, urethral strictures and functional impairment. A well-recognised association exists between MGLSc, differentiated penile intraepithelial neoplasia and PeSCC, representing a carcinogenic pathway that is typically independent of human papillomavirus infection and ultraviolet radiation. Management aims to control inflammation, restore the epithelial barrier and reduce occlusion. Potent topical corticosteroids remain first-line therapy, while circumcision is highly effective when topical therapy fails. Additional medical and surgical treatments may be required in refractory or complicated disease. This review summarizes current knowledge on the clinical features, pathophysiology, malignant potential and management of MGLSc, with emphasis on mechanisms related to occlusion and urinary exposure.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The scope examines male genital lichen sclerosus (MGLSc), a chronic inflammatory dermatosis of the penis, focusing on its clinical manifestations, potential progression to penile squamous cell carcinoma (PeSCC), and the central question of its pathogenesis and management. The disease is characterized by chronic inflammation, scarring, and functional impairment, with a debated aetiology and carcinogenic potential.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes current knowledge on MGLSc, emphasizing mechanisms related to occlusion and urinary exposure. It integrates clinical observations, epidemiological patterns, and management strategies without reporting systematic search methods, eligibility criteria, or study counts.
Consensus and conclusions
Chronic occluded exposure of susceptible epithelium to urine is increasingly supported as a primary aetiological factor in MGLSc. Secondary inflammatory, fibrotic, and epithelial repair processes arise downstream of this insult. Clinically, MGLSc presents with pallor, erythema, fissuring, and scarring, often with subtle urethral abnormalities. Long-standing disease may lead to meatal stenosis, urethral strictures, and functional impairment.
Controversies and gaps
The aetiology of MGLSc remains incompletely understood, with unresolved questions about the relative contributions of chronic urinary exposure versus other potential triggers. The review highlights gaps in understanding the precise mechanisms linking chronic inflammation to malignant transformation, particularly the carcinogenic pathway independent of human papillomavirus infection and ultraviolet radiation.
Research and practice implications
Research priorities include elucidating the molecular and cellular pathways linking chronic urinary exposure to inflammation and fibrosis, and clarifying the mechanisms underlying the progression from MGLSc to PeSCC. Clinically, emphasis should be placed on early diagnosis and intervention to prevent complications such as meatal stenosis and urethral strictures.

阴茎上皮内瘤变和鳞状细胞癌:临床病理特征、分类和预后的当代回顾。

Penile Intraepithelial Neoplasia and Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Contemporary Review of Clinicopathologic Features, Classification, and Prognostication.
作者： Matthew H Lanehart, Eric E Morgan, Colleen J. Beatty, Kody Heubach, Meaghan C. Dougher, Thomas Karrs
摘要中文翻译阴茎上皮内瘤变是一种罕见的癌前生殖器病变,如果没有适当的治疗管理,可能会进展为侵袭性阴茎鳞状细胞癌。最近由于生殖器 发病率 (HPV) 感染负担增加而导致 发病率 增加的趋势强调需要有效的诊断和管理策略。本文提供了临床病理学概述,重点关注先前未总结的肿瘤分类和预后的更新。我们根据 2022 年世界卫生组织指南检查分类和命名法的变化,并探讨 HPV 相关肿瘤和 HPV 独立肿瘤之间 发病机制 和预后的差异。本文强调需要更强有力的分子遗传学研究来进一步阐明预后生物标志物,并探讨免疫组织化学在分层亚型中日益明显的作用。此外,我们还通过一个说明性案例来强调准确的诊断和及时管理的重要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 及其与阴茎上皮内瘤变和鳞状细胞癌的关联,重点关注 HPV 驱动的 人乳头瘤病毒 和肿瘤分类如何为 HPV 相关阴茎病变的预后和管理策略提供信息的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
该文章是一篇当代综述,提供了 HPV 相关阴茎病变的临床病理学概述,强调了基于 2022 年世界卫生组织指南的肿瘤分类和预后的更新。该综述综合了现有文献,没有报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准或纳入研究计数。
共识与结论
该综述强调了 HPV 相关性和 HPV 独立性阴茎肿瘤之间的明显二分法,并更新了 2022 年 WHO 指导预后分层指南的分类和命名法。它强调了免疫组织化学在区分肿瘤亚型中日益重要的作用。
争议与缺口
该综述指出需要进行更强有力的分子遗传学研究来阐明 HPV 相关阴茎病变的预后生物标志物,这表明尚未解决的机制和生物标志物验证方面的差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述优先考虑针对 HPV 相关阴茎病变制定准确的诊断和管理策略,特别是通过分子和免疫组织化学方法的整合来改善亚型分层和预后。

Penile Intraepithelial Neoplasia and Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Contemporary Review of Clinicopathologic Features, Classification, and Prognostication.

Authors: Matthew H Lanehart, Eric E Morgan, Colleen J. Beatty, Kody Heubach, Meaghan C. Dougher, Thomas Karrs
Original abstract (English)
Penile intraepithelial neoplasia is an uncommon precancerous genital lesion that may progress to invasive penile squamous cell carcinoma in the absence of proper therapeutic management. The recent trend of increasing incidence because of a heightened burden of genital human papillomavirus (HPV) infection emphasizes a need for effective diagnostic and management strategies. This article provides a clinicopathologic overview focusing on updates in tumor classification and prognostication not previously summarized. We examine changes in classification and nomenclature based on the 2022 World Health Organization guidelines and explore differences in pathogenesis and prognosis between HPV-associated and HPV-independent tumors. This article emphasizes the need for more robust molecular genetic studies to further elucidate prognostic biomarkers and explores the increasingly distinct role of immunohistochemistry in stratifying subtypes. Also, we present an illustrative case to highlight the significance of accurate diagnosis and prompt management.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomaviruses (HPV) and their association with penile intraepithelial neoplasia and squamous cell carcinoma, focusing on the central question of how HPV-driven pathogenesis and tumor classification inform prognosis and management strategies for HPV-associated penile lesions.
Evidence base and method
The article is a contemporary review providing a clinicopathologic overview of HPV-associated penile lesions, emphasizing updates in tumor classification and prognostication based on the 2022 World Health Organization guidelines. The review synthesizes existing literature without reporting databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights a distinct dichotomy between HPV-associated and HPV-independent penile tumors, with classification and nomenclature updates from the 2022 WHO guidelines guiding prognostic stratification. It underscores the increasing role of immunohistochemistry in differentiating tumor subtypes.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a need for more robust molecular genetic studies to elucidate prognostic biomarkers in HPV-associated penile lesions, indicating unresolved mechanisms and gaps in biomarker validation.
Research and practice implications
The review prioritizes the development of accurate diagnostic and management strategies for HPV-associated penile lesions, particularly through the integration of molecular and immunohistochemical methods to improve subtype stratification and prognostication.

喜马偕尔邦西姆拉选定社区地区 35 岁以上妇女预防 宫颈癌 知识的结构化教学计划的有效性:一项准实验研究

Effectiveness of a Structured Teaching Programme on Knowledge Regarding Prevention of Cervical Cancer among Women >35 Years in Selected Community Areas of Shimla, Himachal Pradesh: A Quasi-Experimental Study
作者： Vibha Negi, Kiran Kumari
摘要中文翻译:背景:宫颈癌 仍然是女性癌症相关发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因之一,尽管在很大程度上可以通过 疫苗接种、及时筛查和癌前病变的早期治疗来预防。低收入和中等收入国家的负担尤其严重,这些国家的认识不足、筛查服务利用率低以及求医行为延迟继续阻碍有效的预防。35岁以上女性是重要的目标人群,因为持续感染HPV、长期接触行为危险因素以及有限地参与常规筛查大大增加了她们患宫颈癌的易感性。通过结构化的教育干预措施提高知识是一项重要的护理策略,可以促进知情决策并鼓励社区一级的预防性健康实践。目的:评估关于预防 宫颈癌 知识的结构化教学计划对居住在喜马偕尔邦西姆拉选定社区地区的 35 岁以上女性的有效性。方法:采用一组准实验前测-后测设计的定量研究方法。该研究包括 70 名年龄超过 35 岁的女性,这些女性是通过概率简单随机抽样从喜马偕尔邦西姆拉选定的社区地区选出的。使用经过广泛审查文献和专家验证后开发的经过验证的结构化知识调查问卷来评估基线知识。预测试后,参与者接受了结构化教学计划,重点关注危险因素、警告信号、人乳头瘤病毒 感染、筛查方法、HPV 疫苗接种、生活方式改变和 宫颈癌 的早期 诊断。教育干预后使用相同的问卷进行后测评估。采用描述性统计数据(包括频率、百分比、平均值、标准差)和使用配对 t 检验的推论统计数据来确定干预措施的有效性。结果:干预前,大多数参与者对宫颈癌的预防表现出中等程度的知识,而只有一小部分参与者拥有足够的知识。结构化教学计划实施后,知识水平有了显着提高,大多数妇女获得了足够的知识,没有人处于不足的类别。平均知识得分从预测期间的 18.59 ± 4.04 (51.6%) 显着提高至后测期间的 24.67 ± 4.35 (68.5%),平均提高了 6.08 分。统计分析表明,测试前和测试后知识分数之间存在高度显着差异(t = 20.601,p < 0.001),证实了教育干预的有效性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
中心问题考察结构化教学计划是否提高了喜马偕尔邦西姆拉 35 岁以上女性对 宫颈癌 预防的了解,重点关注 HPV 相关疾病的预防实践。
证据基础与方法
该综述描述了一项定量单组准实验前测试后测试研究,该研究使用概率简单随机抽样从西姆拉选定的社区地区选择 70 名 35 岁以上的女性。通过经过验证的结构化问卷评估基线和干预后知识,统计分析采用描述性和推理性方法,包括配对 t 检验。
共识与结论
结构化教学计划显着提高了平均知识分数,从测试前的 18.59 ± 4.04 (51.6%) 提高到测试后的 24.67 ± 4.35 (68.5%),平均提高了 6.08 分。测试前和测试后分数之间的差异非常显着(t = 20.601,p < 0.001),表明教育干预有效提高了有关宫颈癌预防的知识。
争议与缺口
该研究没有报告知识的长期保留、行为改变的持久性或对实际筛选或 疫苗接种 吸收的影响。准实验设计缺乏对照组,限制了超出当地背景的因果推断和普遍性。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应评估知识获取的可持续性,并将教育改进转化为可衡量的预防行为,例如筛查参与和 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取。公共卫生计划应将结构化教学干预纳入基于社区的 宫颈癌 预防策略,特别是针对 35 岁以上且事先意识有限的女性。

Effectiveness of a Structured Teaching Programme on Knowledge Regarding Prevention of Cervical Cancer among Women >35 Years in Selected Community Areas of Shimla, Himachal Pradesh: A Quasi-Experimental Study

Authors: Vibha Negi, Kiran Kumari
Original abstract (English)
: Background: Cervical cancer remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related morbidity and mortality among women, despite being largely preventable through vaccination, timely screening, and early management of precancerous lesions. The burden is disproportionately higher in low-and middle-income countries, where inadequate awareness, poor utilization of screening services, and delayed health-seeking behaviour continue to hinder effective prevention. Women above 35 years constitute an important target population because persistent Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection, prolonged exposure to behavioural risk factors, and limited participation in routine screening substantially increase their susceptibility to cervical malignancy. Improving knowledge through structured educational interventions represents an important nursing strategy to promote informed decision-making and encourage preventive health practices at the community level. Aim: To evaluate the effectiveness of a Structured Teaching Programme on knowledge regarding prevention of cervical cancer among women aged more than 35 years residing in selected community areas of Shimla, Himachal Pradesh. Methods: A quantitative research approach using a one-group quasi-experimental pre-test – post-test design was adopted. The study included 70 women aged more than 35 years selected through probability simple random sampling from selected community areas of Shimla, Himachal Pradesh. Baseline knowledge was assessed using a validated structured knowledge questionnaire developed following an extensive review of literature and expert validation. Following the pre-test, participants received a Structured Teaching Programme focusing on risk factors, warning signs, Human Papillomavirus infection, screening methods, HPV vaccination, lifestyle modification, and early diagnosis of cervical cancer. Post-test assessment was conducted after the educational intervention using the same questionnaire. Descriptive statistics including frequency, percentage, mean, standard deviation and inferential statistics using the paired t-test were employed to determine the effectiveness of the intervention. Results: Before the intervention, most participants demonstrated moderate knowledge regarding prevention of cervical cancer, while only a small proportion possessed adequate knowledge. Following the Structured Teaching Programme, a marked improvement in knowledge was observed, with the majority of women achieving adequate knowledge and none remaining in the inadequate category. The mean knowledge score increased significantly from 18.59 ± 4.04 (51.6%) during the pre-test to 24.67 ± 4.35 (68.5%) during the post-test, yielding a mean improvement of 6.08 points. Statistical analysis demonstrated a highly significant difference between pre-test and post-test knowledge scores (t = 20.601, p < 0.001), confirming the effectiveness of the educational intervention.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The central question examines whether a structured teaching programme improves knowledge about cervical cancer prevention among women aged over 35 years in Shimla, Himachal Pradesh, focusing on HPV-associated disease prevention practices.
Evidence base and method
The review describes a quantitative one-group quasi-experimental pre-test–post-test study using probability simple random sampling to select 70 women aged over 35 years from selected community areas of Shimla. Baseline and post-intervention knowledge were assessed with a validated structured questionnaire, and statistical analysis employed descriptive and inferential methods including paired t-tests.
Consensus and conclusions
The structured teaching programme significantly increased mean knowledge scores from 18.59 ± 4.04 (51.6%) pre-test to 24.67 ± 4.35 (68.5%) post-test, with a mean improvement of 6.08 points. The difference between pre-test and post-test scores was highly significant (t = 20.601, p < 0.001), indicating the educational intervention effectively improved knowledge regarding cervical cancer prevention.
Controversies and gaps
The study did not report long-term retention of knowledge, durability of behavioural change, or impact on actual screening or vaccination uptake. The quasi-experimental design lacks a control group, limiting causal inference and generalizability beyond the local context.
Research and practice implications
Future research should evaluate the sustainability of knowledge gains and translate educational improvements into measurable preventive behaviours such as screening participation and HPV vaccination uptake. Public health programmes should integrate structured teaching interventions into community-based cervical cancer prevention strategies, particularly targeting women over 35 years with limited prior awareness.

FOXA1 免疫组织化学高表达与 HPV 阳性宫颈鳞状细胞癌的不良后果相关。

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗系列科普问答 - ndcpa.gov.cn

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报继2025年10月发布的七部委政策之后,中国国家免疫规划将从2025年11月起向年满13岁的女孩提供免费二价HPV 疫苗。此举旨在遏制宫颈癌 发病率的上升——2022年新增病例151,000例,死亡56,000例——主要是由高危类型的持续感染造成的HPV16 和 HPV18。疫苗接种 时间安排在首次性行为之前,以最大限度地保护;临床数据显示,9-14 岁儿童的抗体水平是较年长的 队列研究 的两倍,并且持续至少十年。两剂方案可以与其他儿童 疫苗 一起给药,而不会损失疗效或安全性。错过窗口期的学生可以补习 疫苗接种,入学时不需要 HPV 疫苗接种。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表于2026-08-18;事件背景涵盖2022年发病率数据和2025年政策实施。
地点与对象
中国;目标人群:2011年11月10日以后出生且年满13周岁的女孩;全国范围。
事件
根据《疫苗管理法》和七部委联合通知,中国国家免疫规划(NIP)为13岁女孩增加了二价HPV 疫苗,自2025年11月起生效。
规模、影响与风险
2022年全国数据:新增宫颈癌病例151,000例,死亡56,000例;≥70%的病例由HPV16/18引起;疫苗接种可以预防>70%的宫颈癌;危险因素包括吸烟、过早性行为、多个伴侣、合并感染和免疫抑制。
应对、状态与不确定性
NIP在0个月和6个月时提供免费的两剂注射方案;允许与其他 NIP 疫苗 共同给药;HPV 疫苗接种 不是入学要求;补服 疫苗接种 可用于错过的剂量;国家 监测 和不良事件监测已到位。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗系列科普问答 - ndcpa.gov.cn
News Brief
China’s National Immunization Program will provide free bivalent HPV vaccine to girls turning 13 years old from November 2025, following a seven-ministry policy issued in October 2025. The move aims to curb rising cervical cancer incidence—151,000 new cases and 56,000 deaths in 2022—driven largely by persistent infection with high-risk types HPV16 and HPV18. Vaccination is timed before sexual debut to maximize protection; clinical data show antibody levels in 9–14-year-olds are twice those in older cohorts and persist for at least a decade. The two-dose schedule can be administered alongside other childhood vaccines without loss of efficacy or safety. Catch-up vaccination is available for those who miss the window, and HPV vaccination is not required for school entry.
Original source text (Chinese)

宫颈癌是女性最常见的恶性肿瘤之一。国家癌症中心发布的数据显示,2022年我国宫颈癌新发病例为15.1万例,死亡病例是5.6万例,严重威胁女性身心健康。对宫颈癌分年龄段分析发现,发病率在20岁之前处于较低水平,20岁以后逐渐上升,至50—54岁年龄组达高峰,之后逐渐下降。近20年来,我国宫颈癌的发病率呈上升且年轻化的趋势。宫颈癌病因明确,主要是由高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)持续感染导致的。感染高危型HPV并不等于得宫颈癌,但如果持续感染,就有可能发生上皮内瘤样病变,或者进一步发展为宫颈癌。 发生HPV持续感染,还有一些高危因素,如吸烟、初次性行为年龄过早、多个性伴侣、感染其他疾病、免疫功能低下(如HIV感染者、器官/骨髓移植后患者、自身免疫性疾病患者、长期服用免疫抑制剂患者等)。具有这些因素的人群更容易发生HPV持续感染,进而患宫颈癌。 二、为适龄儿童免费提供国家免疫规划HPV疫苗接种服务,有哪些依据和意义? 《中华人民共和国疫苗管理法》规定,国家实行免疫规划制度,政府免费向居民提供免疫规划疫苗。2025年10月,国家疾控局、国家卫生健康委等七部门联合印发《关于将人乳头瘤病毒疫苗纳入国家免疫规划有关工作事宜的通知》,明确将HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,各地为2011年11月10日以后出生的满13周岁女孩免费接种2剂次双价HPV疫苗。 研究表明,广泛接种疫苗,可以有效预防70%以上的宫颈癌发生,同时也可以降低因感染HPV导致的其他疾病的发生。其他国家和地区经验显示,将HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,能够有效保障不同地区、不同收入水平的目标人群公平地获得预防接种服务,促进健康公平,提升疫苗接种率。我国将HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,是坚持以人民为中心发展思想、贯彻落实健康优先发展战略的具体行动,是降低人群疾病负担、保障群众身体健康的惠民利民措施。 三、我国将HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,确定13周岁女孩作为接种目标人群的考虑是什么? 自2025年11月起,我国将HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,13周岁女孩作为目标人群,是综合考虑HPV感染风险、疫苗保护效果、成本效益以及实施可行性等因素作出的最优选择。 一是在HPV感染几率增大前,也就是发生初次性行为前,进行HPV疫苗接种,可以最大限度发挥疫苗预防HPV感染的作用,以获得更好的保护效果。二是国内外研究表明,9—14周岁女孩接种HPV疫苗产生的抗体水平,是15周岁以上人群的2倍以上,且保护效果可持续至少10年以上。在13周岁接种疫苗,可以获得更高的抗体水平和更持久的保护效果。三是全球为适龄女孩免费接种HPV疫苗的国家,目标人群多为11—13周岁女孩。我国将HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划之前,广东、江西、海南等省份通过民生项目等形式,为初中适龄女孩免费接种HPV疫苗,接种覆盖人群超千万,验证了将13周岁女孩作为接种目标人群的可行性。 四、为什么将双价HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划?如果已经接种四价、九价等其他价次的HPV疫苗,是否还需要接种免费的双价疫苗? 研究表明,至少70%的宫颈癌与HPV16型、18型持续感染有关,双价HPV疫苗可预防这两种型别的病毒感染,在预防由HPV感染引起的宫颈癌及癌前病变等方面具有较好的免疫效果。现阶段,我国将双价HPV疫苗纳入国家免疫规划,是综合考虑HPV感染及疾病负担、疫苗有效性和安全性、疫苗生产供应情况、财政可负担等多方面因素确定的。 如果适龄女孩已自费接种四价、九价HPV疫苗,也视同完成了免疫规划疫苗接种,不需要重复接种。四价、九价HPV疫苗除了预防宫颈癌外,还可以预防尖锐湿疣等疾病发生。适龄儿童家长可以综合考虑孩子的健康需求等实际情况,选择接种免费的国家免疫规划疫苗,或自费接种四价、九价疫苗。 五、纳入国家免疫规划的HPV疫苗接种程序如何?HPV疫苗可以和其他疫苗一起接种吗? 为2011年11月10日以后出生的、年龄满13周岁的女孩免费接种双价HPV疫苗,一共需要接种两剂次。完成首剂接种后,间隔半年接种第二剂次,间隔期最好不要超过一年。 临床研究证据证明,HPV疫苗和其他免疫规划疫苗(如甲肝疫苗、乙肝疫苗、百白破疫苗、脊髓灰质炎疫苗、流脑疫苗等)同时接种,没有发现有效性降低、安全性风险增加的情景。国际共识是:HPV疫苗可以与其他疫苗同时接种。如同时接种疫苗,要使用不同的注射器、选择不同的部位进行接种,如在左上臂接种HPV疫苗,可在右上臂接种其他疫苗。 六、HPV疫苗属于预防接种证查验的范围吗?未接种HPV疫苗是否影响入学? 《中华人民共和国疫苗管理法》规定,居住在中国境内的居民,依法享有接种免疫规划疫苗的权利,履行接种免疫规划疫苗的义务。HPV疫苗已纳入国家免疫规划,适龄女孩应当履行疫苗接种义务,监护人应当依法保证适龄女孩按时接种。 按照《儿童入托、入学预防接种证查验办法》规定,儿童入托、入学时,托幼机构、学校应当查验预防接种证。现阶段,全国所有托育机构、幼儿园和小学均应开展入托、入学预防接种证查验工作。其他类型学校是否纳入查验管理,由当地有关部门根据疾病防控需要确定。部分地区疾控、卫生健康、教育部门已将适龄女孩HPV疫苗接种情况纳入预防接种证查验内容,通过查验预防接种证,核实孩子疫苗接种情况,给家长和孩子提供健康提示,及时发现并补种漏种疫苗,保障儿童健康和校园公共卫生安全。适龄儿童家长应积极配合开展预防接种证查验工作,需要补种疫苗的及时补种。但是否完成HPV疫苗接种不是入学门槛,不会影响孩子入学。 七、感染了HPV后还需要接种疫苗吗?打完疫苗是否就一劳永逸了? HPV有200多种型别,如个体检测发现感染了HPV,可能感染了其中某一种或几种型别的病毒。对于个体来讲,即使已感染某一种或几种特定型别的HPV,接种疫苗仍然可以有效预防未感染的其他HPV亚型(疫苗包含型别)。 HPV疫苗属于预防性疫苗,对已经发生的病毒感染无清除作用,如已发生病变,也不能阻止病变的进展;即使接种疫苗,仍建议定期进行宫颈癌筛查,并保持健康的生活方式。部分人群因各种原因没有接种HPV疫苗,患病风险相对较高,需要定期进行宫颈癌筛查。 八、接种HPV疫苗有哪些注意事项? 国家免疫规划HPV疫苗的接种对象为未成年人,接种疫苗通常需要监护人(父亲、母亲或其他法定监护人)的陪同。接种前,建议孩子和家长提前了解HPV疫苗及其针对疾病的相关知识,以及接种的流程。接种时,监护人要携带本人及孩子身份证件(如身份证、户口本)、孩子的预防接种证,仔细阅读知情同意书,配合现场预防接种工作人员询问,详细提供孩子的健康状况以及既往的疾病史、过敏史、接种禁忌等情况,由接种医生来判断本次是否可以接种。如在学校设立的临时接种点接种疫苗,学生要提供监护人签署的知情同意书,再进行疫苗接种。 接种HPV疫苗后,可以用棉签轻轻按住接种部位,避免揉搓,并留观30分钟,以便及时发现和处理可能出现的不良反应。如果出现不适症状,需及时告知接种单位工作人员进行处理。回家后适当休息,保持接种部位清洁。如出现注射部位疼痛、红肿等局部反应,通常居家处理即可,如冷敷缓解疼痛等。如出现低热、乏力、食欲减退等情况,注意观察、多休息。如出现严重不适等情形,建议及时请医生诊治。 九、免费接种的双价HPV疫苗,在接种安全方面有哪些保障措施? 纳入国家免疫规划的国产双价HPV疫苗,已上市使用多年,已在十余个国家适龄儿童中接种,其安全性、有效性已得到充分验证。疫苗在研制、上市审批、生产和批签发、流通、预防接种全过程都有严格、规范的监管措施。在接种疫苗时,接种人员要严格按照《预防接种工作规范(2023年版)》实施操作,严格执行“三查七对一验证”、接种后30分钟留观等要求,保障接种工作的规范性和安全性。 十、错过了13岁免费接种时间,是否可以补种?已经自费打过一针,还能享受免费接种政策吗? 各地接种单位常态化开展免疫规划疫苗的查漏补种。符合出生日期要求的适龄女孩,如果因各种原因错过了HPV疫苗免费接种时间,可以到辖区接种点免费进行补种。建议家长及时带孩子到接种单位进行补种。已自费接种部分剂次的人群,可按原有程序完成全程接种。 扫一扫在手机打开当前页 手机版 微信公众号 国家疾病预防控制局 版权所有电脑端
View five news elements
Time
Published on 2026-08-18; event context spans 2022 incidence data and 2025 policy implementation.
Location and population
China; target population: girls born after 2011-11-10 who reach 13 years of age; national scope.
Event
China’s National Immunization Program (NIP) added bivalent HPV vaccine for 13-year-old girls, effective from November 2025, based on the Vaccine Administration Law and a seven-ministry joint notice.
Scale, impact and risk
2022 national data: 151,000 new cervical cancer cases and 56,000 deaths; HPV16/18 cause ≥70% of cases; vaccination can prevent >70% of cervical cancer; risk factors include smoking, early sexual debut, multiple partners, co-infections, and immunosuppression.
Response, status and uncertainty
NIP provides free two-dose schedule at 0 and 6 months; co-administration with other NIP vaccines is permitted; HPV vaccination is not a school-entry requirement; catch-up vaccination is available for missed doses; national surveillance and adverse-event monitoring are in place.

What Families Should Know about the Human papillomavirus (HPV) - checkupnewsroom.com

News Brief
The article titled 'What Families Should Know about the Human papillomavirus (HPV)' published on August 17, 2026, by checkupnewsroom.com provides general information about HPV and emphasizes the importance of immunizations for children. It mentions that navigating vaccines can feel overwhelming for parents and suggests consulting a pediatrician for guidance. However, the article does not report any confirmed cases, deaths, or specific health impacts related to HPV. The content appears to be an informational piece aimed at educating families about the virus and its prevention, without detailing any outbreak or public health emergency. The article is part of a broader series on child health and safety, highlighting the role of healthcare systems in providing resources and support. No specific risk levels or response measures are mentioned in the evidence provided.

HPV 自检扩大了未筛查人群的 宫颈癌 筛查 - 临床实验室产品

HPV Self-Testing Expands Cervical Cancer Screening in Underscreened Population - Clinical Lab Products
新闻简报《JAMA Network Open》上发表的一项研究强调了 HPV 自检在提高筛查不足人群中 宫颈癌 筛查率方面的有效性,特别是在阿片类药物 治疗 项目中。该倡议报告称,90% 的符合条件的患者选择接受筛查,这表明获取和参与的情况显着改善。证据的重点是实施自我测试作为解决 宫颈癌 筛查差距的方法。证据中没有提供具体的病例数、死亡人数或风险水平。这些发现是根据研究结果得到证实的,报告没有提及任何正在进行的调查或不确定性。这一进展是改善诊断技术和扩大 HPV 相关疾病筛查范围的更广泛努力的一部分。
查看新闻五要素
时间
分析显示血液 RNA 测试可改善乳腺癌筛查结果和成本效益 乳房遗传风险评分可识别 MODY 转诊中隐藏的 1 型糖尿病 糖尿病和代谢性疾病 GI-MAP 为测试结果呈阳性的患者添加环孢子虫指导 胃肠道感染 FDA 批准对 40 岁以下的成年人进行阿尔茨海默氏症血液检测 痴呆症和阿尔茨海默氏症,质谱 cfDNA 筛查显示高无需伴侣检测即可提高胎儿风险的准确性 产前检测 2,000 名患者肺癌项目转向液体活检进行基因组筛查 肺癌、分子诊断研究人员开发 AI 框架以提高病理诊断可靠性 解剖病理学、癌症、数字病理学 患者可以在医生面前看到实验室结果。
地点与对象
筛查不足的人群,特别是在阿片类药物 治疗 计划中。
事件
根据 JAMA Network Open 的一项研究,实施 HPV 自检是为了扩大 宫颈癌 筛查范围,90% 的符合条件的患者选择参加。
规模、影响与风险
该研究报告称,符合条件的患者的筛查率显着增加,但证据中没有提供具体的病例数、死亡数或风险水平。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究被报告为一项方法学发现,没有提及调查、测试、隔离或接触者追踪。该证据基于已发表的研究,并且研究结果已得到证实。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

HPV Self-Testing Expands Cervical Cancer Screening in Underscreened Population - Clinical Lab Products

News Brief
A study published in JAMA Network Open highlights the effectiveness of HPV self-testing in increasing cervical cancer screening rates among underscreened populations, particularly at an opioid treatment program. The initiative reported that 90% of eligible patients opted in for screening, indicating a significant improvement in access and participation. The evidence focuses on the implementation of self-testing as a methodological approach to address gaps in cervical cancer screening. No specific case counts, death counts, or risk levels were provided in the evidence. The findings are confirmed based on the study's results, and the report does not mention any ongoing investigations or uncertainties. This development is part of broader efforts to improve diagnostic technologies and expand screening for HPV-associated diseases.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

宫颈癌:抗氧化剂摄入量越高,几率降低 56% - 今日医学新闻

Cervical cancer: Higher antioxidant intake linked to 56% lower odds - Medical News Today
新闻简报《今日医学新闻》于 2026 年 8 月 21 日报道的《国际妇产科杂志》上发表的一项研究发现,膳食抗氧化剂摄入量较高,包括维生素 A、C、E、锌、硒和类胡萝卜素,与 患病率 较低相关。该研究分析了国家健康和营养检查调查 (NHANES) 的数据,涉及 1,044 名参与者,其中 174 人自我报告有 宫颈癌 病史。研究发现,与摄入量最低的参与者相比,抗氧化剂摄入量最高的参与者有 宫颈癌 病史的几率低约 56%。然而,研究结果并未确定因果关系,因为该研究是横断面研究,无法确定饮食是否影响癌症的发展,或者参与者在 诊断 后是否改变了饮食。作者指出,虽然抗氧化剂可能有助于防止氧化应激和炎症,从而导致 HPV 感染和 宫颈癌 的发展,但还需要更多的研究来证实这种联系。他们还强调,HPV 疫苗接种 和 宫颈癌 筛查仍然是预防的基础,饮食应该补充而不是取代这些措施。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2026年8月21日发表。数据分析基于横断面设计,事件日期为饮食回忆访谈和宫颈癌历史评估的时间。
地点与对象
该研究在美国进行,参与国家健康和营养检查调查 (NHANES) 的参与者,包括 1,044 人,其中 174 人有自我报告的 宫颈癌 病史,870 人没有。
事件
《国际妇产科杂志》上发表的一项研究发现,较高的膳食抗氧化剂摄入量与较低的 患病率 之间存在关联,但并未确定因果关系。《今日医学新闻》报道了这项研究。
规模、影响与风险
该研究发现,与摄入抗氧化剂最低的参与者相比,摄入抗氧化剂最高的参与者患宫颈癌的可能性约低56%。然而,研究结果并未表明摄入更多抗氧化剂可预防宫颈癌。研究还指出,宫颈癌是全球重要的健康问题,2020年全球报告病例超过600,000例,死亡342,000例。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是观察性和横断面研究,这意味着它无法确定饮食是否影响癌症的发展,或者参与者是否在 诊断 后改变了饮食。作者强调,未来需要进行纵向研究,以更好地了解抗氧化剂摄入量与 宫颈癌 发育之间的关系。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Cervical cancer: Higher antioxidant intake linked to 56% lower odds - Medical News Today

News Brief
A study published in the International Journal of Gynecology & Obstetrics, reported by Medical News Today on August 21, 2026, found that higher dietary antioxidant intake, including vitamins A, C, E, zinc, selenium, and carotenoids, was associated with a lower prevalence of cervical cancer. The research analyzed data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES), involving 1,044 participants, of whom 174 had a self-reported history of cervical cancer. The study found that participants with the highest antioxidant intake had about 56% lower odds of having a history of cervical cancer compared to those with the lowest intake. However, the findings do not establish causation, as the study was cross-sectional and could not determine whether diet influenced cancer development or if participants changed their diets after diagnosis. The authors noted that while antioxidants may help protect against oxidative stress and inflammation, which could contribute to HPV infection and cervical cancer development, more research is needed to confirm this link. They also emphasized that HPV vaccination and cervical cancer screening remain the foundation of prevention, and diet should complement, not replace, these measures.
Original source text (English)
Study finds link between antioxidant-rich diets and lower cervical cancer odds Written by Erika Watts on August 21, 2026 — Fact checked by Jill Seladi-Schulman, Ph.D. Could a diet high in antioxidants help prevent cervical cancer? istetiana/Getty Images Researchers examined whether higher dietary antioxidant intake, including vitamins A and C and zinc, was associated with the prevalence of cervical cancer. They analyzed data from more than 1,000 women who participated in a large U.S. health and nutrition survey. Women with the highest antioxidant intake had about 56% lower odds of having a history of cervical cancer compared with those with the lowest intake. Researchers say more studies are needed to better understand the relationship between antioxidant intake and cervical cancer risk. Cervical cancer, which is primarily caused by persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), is a significant global health concern. Cervical cancer is less common in the U.S., but is one of the most common cancers that affects women worldwide, especially in lower-income countries. A new study examined the relationship between dietary antioxidant intake and cervical cancer and found that higher antioxidant intake was associated with a lower prevalence of the disease. The study was published in the International Journal of Gynecology & Obstetrics. How researchers measured dietary antioxidant intake There were more than 600,000 cases of cervical cancer and about 342,000 deaths from the disease worldwide in 2020. Although HPV vaccination and cervical cancer screening can help prevent the disease, access to these preventive measures is uneven, particularly in lower-income countries. Researchers are also interested in identifying additional modifiable factors that may contribute to the development of cervical cancer, including nutrition. For the new study, scientists analyzed data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES), a program that assesses the health and nutritional status of people in the U.S. The researchers included 1,044 participants in their analysis. Of these, 174 had a self-reported history of cervical cancer, and 870 participants without cervical cancer served as matched controls. The team analyzed the participants’ diets using two nonconsecutive 24-hour dietary recall interviews. They then calculated each participant’s Composite Dietary Antioxidant Index (CDAI). The CDAI provides an overall measure of dietary antioxidant intake based on six components: vitamins A, C, E zinc selenium carotenoids Higher CDAI scores indicate higher overall dietary antioxidant intake. Antioxidants may help protect against oxidative stress and inflammation, which the researchers thought may contribute to HPV infection and cervical cancer development. The researchers used CDAI scores to examine whether overall antioxidant intake was associated with the prevalence of cervical cancer. They also accounted for factors such as age, race and ethnicity, income, smoking and alcohol use, and BMI. Higher antioxidant intake linked to lower cervical cancer prevalence After accounting for these factors, the researchers found that each 1-unit increase in CDAI score was associated with 11.4% lower odds of having a history of cervical cancer. Participants with the highest dietary antioxidant intake had about 56% lower odds of having a history of cervical cancer than those with the lowest intake. The study authors said that antioxidants may affect oxidative stress, inflammation, and immune responses, which could help explain the association. However, the findings do not show that a higher antioxidant intake prevents cervical cancer. The study had several limitations, including its cross-sectional design. Since the researchers assessed participants’ diets and cervical cancer history at a single point in time, they could not determine whether diet influenced cancer development or whether participants changed their diets after diagnosis. Participants also self-reported their cervical cancer diagnoses, and researchers did not have information about cervical cancer stage at diagnosis. The authors say that future studies should follow participants’ diets over time to better understand whether antioxidant intake affects cervical cancer development. Can antioxidants help with cervical cancer prevention? Jagdish Khubchandani, PhD, MPH, a public health professor at New Mexico State University, shared his thoughts on the study with Medical News Today. Khubchandani noted that while studies using NHANES data can provide valuable insights, dietary research has limitations, including relying on participants accurately recalling what they eat. However, he said there are several biological mechanisms that could help explain the findings. “Antioxidants in many dietary items (e.g., fruits and veggies) neutralize these toxins that can damage DNA in cells,” Khubchandani explained. “Furthermore, antioxidants, vitamins, and minerals in healthy diets also protect our tumor-suppressing proteins.” “The science is well established that healthy diets reduce the risk of cancers,” he said, adding that greater attention should be paid to improving “access, cost, and lifestyles.” 5 servings of fruit and veg a day as a starting point Khubchandani recommends aiming for five servings of fruits and vegetables daily when possible, while emphasizing that diet cannot prevent HPV infection when other risk factors are present. “Good diets can ensure a strong immune response to any infection,” Khubchandani emphasized. Laurie Brunette-Masi, MD, a board certified gynecologic oncologist at City of Hope Orange County in Irvine, California, told MNT that the findings should be interpreted cautiously. “It is important, however, not to interpret this as proof that antioxidants prevent cervical cancer,” said Brunette-Masi. “This was an observational study that shows an association, not cause and effect.” Brunette-Masi noted that antioxidants are part of a healthy lifestyle and may help support immune function, but emphasized that HPV vaccination and recommended cervical cancer screening are “the foundation” of cervical cancer prevention. “A healthy diet may be another piece of the prevention picture, but it should complement, not replace, these proven measures,” said Brunette-Masi. Cervical Cancer / HPV Vaccine Nutrition / Diet Women's Health / Gynecology How we reviewed this article: History Share this article
View five news elements
Time
The study was published on August 21, 2026. The data analysis was based on a cross-sectional design, with the event date being the time of the dietary recall interviews and cervical cancer history assessment.
Location and population
The study was conducted in the U.S. and involved participants from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES), including 1,044 individuals with 174 having a self-reported history of cervical cancer and 870 without.
Event
A study published in the International Journal of Gynecology & Obstetrics found an association between higher dietary antioxidant intake and lower prevalence of cervical cancer, but it did not establish causation. The study was reported by Medical News Today.
Scale, impact and risk
The study found that participants with the highest dietary antioxidant intake had about 56% lower odds of having a history of cervical cancer compared with those with the lowest intake. However, the findings do not show that higher antioxidant intake prevents cervical cancer. The study also noted that cervical cancer is a significant global health concern, with over 600,000 cases and 342,000 deaths worldwide in 2020.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was observational and cross-sectional, meaning it could not determine whether diet influenced cancer development or whether participants changed their diets after diagnosis. The authors emphasized the need for future longitudinal studies to better understand the relationship between antioxidant intake and cervical cancer development.
原文 / Source
Published: 2026-08-20  |  Publisher: Medscape

HPV 疫苗接种 在移植受者中未得到充分利用 - Medscape

HPV Vaccination Underutilized in Transplant Recipients - Medscape
新闻简报2026 年 8 月 7 日发表在 JAAD Reviews 上的一篇综述强调,实体器官移植受者 (SOTR) 中的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 率仍然较低,尽管他们患皮肤鳞状细胞癌 (cSCC) 的风险增加,并且 HPV 与移植后 cSCC 之间存在联系。该研究分析了 2006 年 1 月至 2025 年 8 月期间发表的 16 项研究,发现 SOTR,尤其是成年人,疫苗接种 的吸收率一直较低。HPV 6、11、16 和 18 型的血清转化率范围为 52% 至 90%,显着低于在免疫功能正常的个体中观察到的超过 99% 的血清转化率。虽然四项研究的疗效数据表明 疫苗接种 可以减少 HPV 相关病变负担,包括减少宫颈并发症和减少疣复发,但证据受到小型、异质性研究的限制,无法确定因果关系。作者建议采取有针对性的干预措施,例如药剂师主导的咨询和结构化传染病咨询,以提高 SOTR 中的 疫苗接种 率。
查看新闻五要素
时间
审查时间为2006年1月至2025年8月,文章发表于2026年8月20日。
地点与对象
不同国家和人口统计的实体器官移植受者 (SOTR),包括儿童和成人。
事件
对 16 项研究的系统评价显示,尽管 SOTR 患皮肤鳞状细胞癌 (cSCC) 的风险较高,而且 HPV 与移植后 cSCC 存在关联,但 SOTR 中的 HPV 疫苗接种 率仍然较低。
规模、影响与风险
SOTR 队列研究(六项研究)中的 HPV 疫苗接种 率始终较低,尤其是在成人中。HPV 6、11、16 和 18 的血清转化率在 52% 至 90% 之间,而免疫功能正常的个体则超过 99%。功效数据表明,发病率 可减少 HPV 相关病变负担,包括减少宫颈并发症、减少角质形成细胞癌 发病率 以及减少疣复发。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究于 2026 年 8 月 7 日在线发表,功效证据受到小型、异质性研究的限制,无法确定因果关系。作者强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来提高 疫苗接种 的吸收,并指出移植后 疫苗接种 会引起减弱但可测量的 免疫应答。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

HPV Vaccination Underutilized in Transplant Recipients - Medscape

News Brief
A review published in JAAD Reviews on August 7, 2026, highlights that human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination rates remain low in solid organ transplant recipients (SOTRs), despite their increased risk for cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (cSCC) and the link between HPV and post-transplant cSCC. The study, which analyzed 16 studies published from January 2006 through August 2025, found that SOTRs, especially adults, had consistently low vaccination uptake. Seroconversion rates for HPV types 6, 11, 16, and 18 ranged from 52% to 90%, significantly lower than the over 99% observed in immunocompetent individuals. While efficacy data from four studies suggested reductions in HPV-associated lesion burden with vaccination, including fewer cervical complications and reduced wart recurrence, the evidence was limited by small, heterogeneous studies that could not establish causality. The authors recommended targeted interventions, such as pharmacist-led counseling and structured infectious disease consultations, to improve vaccination rates among SOTRs.
Original source text (English)
Medscape AI News & Perspective Tools & Reference CME/CE More EN RegisterLog In AI Mode Medscape Medical NewsMedBrief Medbrief HPV Vaccination Underutilized in Solid Organ Transplant Recipients Edited by Deepa Varma August 20, 2026 Add to Email Alerts TOPLINE Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination rates remain low in solid organ transplant recipients (SOTRs), despite their elevated risk for cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (cSCC), and the association of HPV with post-transplant cSCC, a review shows. METHODOLOGY Researchers conducted a systematic review of 16 studies involving pediatric and adult SOTRs published from January 2006 through August 2025. Two reviewers independently screened articles, extracted data, and assessed quality using Grading of Recommendations, Assessment, Development, and Evaluations and Joanna Briggs Institute tools, with discrepancies adjudicated by a third reviewer. Studies were categorized according to vaccination uptake, immunogenicity, or HPV-associated lesion outcomes. TAKEAWAY HPV vaccination rates were consistently low in SOTR cohorts (six studies), particularly among adults; pharmacist-led counseling and structured infectious disease consultations increased uptake. Seroconversion rates for HPV 6, 11, 16, and 18 ranged from 52% to 90% (six studies), compared with more than 99% observed in immunocompetent individuals; HPV16 seroconversion was among the highest across cohorts. Greater immunosuppression, including higher tacrolimus levels, mycophenolate use, lung transplant status, and earlier post-transplant vaccination, predicted lower immune response to HPV vaccination. Efficacy data from four studies suggested reductions in HPV-associated lesion burden with vaccination, including fewer cervical complications, reduction in keratinocyte carcinoma incidence, resolution of squamous cell carcinoma in situ, and decreased wart recurrence. IN PRACTICE "HPV vaccination is underutilized in SOTRs, though targeted interventions may improve uptake," the authors wrote. "Post-transplant vaccination elicits attenuated but measurable immune responses, influenced by immunosuppression intensity and timing," they added. SOURCE The study was led by Elizabeth Fan, BA, Duke University School of Medicine, Durham, North Carolina, and was published online on August 7 in JAAD Reviews. LIMITATIONS The efficacy evidence was limited by small, heterogeneous studies that could not establish causality. DISCLOSURES The study received no funding. The authors disclosed having no relevant conflicts of interest. SUGGESTED FOR YOU This article was created using several editorial tools, including AI, as part of the process. Human editors reviewed this content before publication. Solid organ transplant recipients (SOTRs) across various countries and demographics, including pediatric and adult populations.
Event
HPV vaccination rates remain low in SOTRs, despite their elevated risk for cutaneous squamous cell carcinoma (cSCC) and the association of HPV with post-transplant cSCC, as shown in a systematic review of 16 studies.
Scale, impact and risk
HPV vaccination rates were consistently low in SOTR cohorts (six studies), particularly among adults. Seroconversion rates for HPV 6, 11, 16, and 18 ranged from 52% to 90%, compared with more than 99% in immunocompetent individuals. Efficacy data suggested reductions in HPV-associated lesion burden with vaccination, including fewer cervical complications, reduction in keratinocyte carcinoma incidence, and decreased wart recurrence.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

研究表明,接种 HPV 疫苗的人患头颈癌的风险较低

People vaccinated against HPV have lower risk of head and neck cancer, study suggests
新闻简报A study published in the International Journal of Infectious Diseases on August 21, 2026, suggests that people vaccinated against human papillomavirus (HPV) have a lower risk of developing head and neck cancer. The research analyzed 1.4 million medical records from 66 US healthcare organizations, with participants vaccinated from ages 9 to 45. Those vaccinated between ages 9 and 26 were 68% less likely to be diagnosed with head or neck cancer, while no significant reduction was observed in those vaccinated between 27 and 45, likely due to prior HPV exposure. The study also found that HPV vaccination was associated with a 91% lower likelihood of oropharyngeal cancer, which includes the tonsils, base of the tongue, and throat. However, the small number of cases (40 among vaccinated individuals and 130 in the comparison group) and the 13-year follow-up period raise statistical uncertainty. Researchers emphasized that the findings should be viewed as an observational association rather than a definitive causal link. They called for larger studies and longer follow-up to better understand the vaccine's impact on cancer prevention. The study highlights HPV's established role in causing oropharyngeal cancer and supports ongoing efforts to monitor its effects over time.
查看新闻五要素
时间
The study was published on 2026-08-21. The research analyzed medical records from January 2011 to June 2024, with follow-up for 13 years after vaccination.
地点与对象
The study involved 1.4 million medical records from 66 US healthcare organizations. Participants included individuals vaccinated against HPV from ages 9 to 45, and those vaccinated against other diseases but not HPV.
事件
《国际传染病杂志》上发表的一项研究表明,接种 HPV 疫苗的人患头颈癌的风险较低。研究结果证实了早期关于这种关联的研究,并表明在 9 至 26 岁之间接种疫苗的人患头颈癌的可能性降低了 68%,而在 27 岁至 45 岁之间接种疫苗的人没有显着降低。
规模、影响与风险
研究发现,HPV 疫苗接种 与口咽癌的可能性降低 91% 相关。然而,诊断出的头颈癌总数很少(接种疫苗的个体中有 40 例,对照组有 130 例),因此很难估计确切的风险降低程度。该研究还指出,现在大多数头颈癌都是由 HPV 引起的,并且这种保护性关联在癌症亚位点和亚组中持续存在。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究的结果被描述为观察关联,而不是对 疫苗 有效性的精确因果估计。独立科学家强调,由于病例数量较少,统计上存在不确定性。研究人员承认,13 年的随访时间可能还不够长,不足以全面评估癌症风险。该研究的局限性包括需要更大规模的研究和更长的随访时间来确认 HPV 疫苗接种 对癌症预防的确切影响。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

People vaccinated against HPV have lower risk of head and neck cancer, study suggests

News Brief
A study published in the International Journal of Infectious Diseases on August 21, 2026, suggests that people vaccinated against human papillomavirus (HPV) have a lower risk of developing head and neck cancer. The research analyzed 1.4 million medical records from 66 US healthcare organizations, with participants vaccinated from ages 9 to 45. Those vaccinated between ages 9 and 26 were 68% less likely to be diagnosed with head or neck cancer, while no significant reduction was observed in those vaccinated between 27 and 45, likely due to prior HPV exposure. The study also found that HPV vaccination was associated with a 91% lower likelihood of oropharyngeal cancer, which includes the tonsils, base of the tongue, and throat. However, the small number of cases (40 among vaccinated individuals and 130 in the comparison group) and the 13-year follow-up period raise statistical uncertainty. Researchers emphasized that the findings should be viewed as an observational association rather than a definitive causal link. They called for larger studies and longer follow-up to better understand the vaccine's impact on cancer prevention. The study highlights HPV's established role in causing oropharyngeal cancer and supports ongoing efforts to monitor its effects over time.
Original source text (English)

Topics & Projects Antimicrobial Stewardship Chronic Wasting Disease Osterholm Update Podcasts Newsletters About CIDRAP CIDRAP in the News Our Staff Our Director Osterholm in the Press Shop Merchandise Contact Us About Search CLOSE Search Support Menu People vaccinated against HPV have lower risk of head and neck cancer, study suggests Liz Szabo, MA Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Heather Hazzan / Self magazine Share Copied to clipboard People vaccinated against the human papillomavirus (HPV) were much less likely to develop cancers of the head and neck, according to a study published this week in the International Journal of Infectious Diseases. The study confirms earlier research finding a vaccine-related decrease in head and neck cancers. The new study analyzed 1.4 million medical records from 66 US healthcare organizations. Half of the research subjects were vaccinated against HPV from ages 9 to 45. The other half were vaccinated against other diseases but not HPV. Study participants were vaccinated from January 2011 to June 2024. People vaccinated from age 9 to 26 years—the age range recommended by the American Academy of Pediatrics—were 68% less likely to be diagnosed as having head or neck cancer, according to the study. There was no statistically significant reduction in head and neck cancers in people vaccinated from age 27 to 45, most likely because these people had been exposed to HPV before being immunized, said Ching-Nung Wu, MD, PhD, first author of the paper and an otolaryngologist at Kaohsiung Chang Gung Memorial Hospital in Taiwan. A common cause of cancer HPV causes nearly all cases of cervical cancer, along with more than 90% of anal cancers, 75% of vaginal cancers, 70% of vulvar and head and neck cancers, and 63% of penile cancers. A study published in December 2025 found the vaccine reduces the risk of tumors of the vagina and vulva. A study published in April found that men and boys who received the HPV vaccine from ages nine to 26 were nearly 50% less likely to develop cancers of the head and neck, esophagus, anus, or penis. Although head and neck cancers were once caused mostly by tobacco and alcohol use, most of these tumors are now caused by HPV. Cancers related to HPV tend to develop in an area called the oropharynx, which includes the tonsils, base of the tongue, back part of the roof of the mouth, and the side and back walls of the throat. In the new study, people vaccinated against HPV were 91% less likely to be diagnosed as having oropharyngeal cancers. Statistical uncertainty Independent scientists note that the total number of head and neck cancers diagnosed among study participants was very small, with 40 among people vaccinated against HPV and 130 in the comparison group. Oropharyngeal cancers made up a fraction of these tumors. That makes it tricky to estimate the exact reduction in risk, said Taito Kitano, DrPH, a researcher at Nara Prefecture General Medical Center in Nara, Japan, who was not involved in the new study. Although Wu and his colleagues followed up with study participants for 13 years after the patients were vaccinated, that may not have been a long enough time to judge their cancer risk. Head and neck cancers are typically diagnosed in older men, at an average age of 64, according to the American Cancer Society. “These figures should be read as an observational association, not a precise causal estimate of vaccine effectiveness,” Wu told CIDRAP News. These figures should be read as an observational association, not a precise causal estimate of vaccine effectiveness. “What gives us confidence in the overall direction, rather than the exact magnitude, is the consistency of the findings: The protective association held across cancer subsites, across subgroups, and persisted five to 13 years after vaccination, and it is biologically plausible given HPV's established causal role in oropharyngeal cancer.” Larger study needed To truly understand how many oropharyngeal cancers are prevented with HPV vaccines, Kitano said scientists would have to perform a larger study and follow participants for a longer time. In spite of the study’s limitations, “it is a good trend and something we should continue to follow over time,” said Mark Einstein, MD, chair of obstetrics and gynecology and women’s health at Montefiore Medical Center in New York. 女子感染的是哪种HPV类型,会致癌吗? - 新浪财经

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报中国武汉一名28岁女性因多次使用共用瑜伽垫后被诊断出患有低危皮肤HPV感染引起的扁平疣。卫生当局澄清,所涉及的菌株与与 宫颈癌 相关的 高危型HPV 类型无关,并且不构成致癌风险。这种感染是良性的,可以通过标准皮肤病程序(例如冷冻疗法或激光疗法)进行治疗。公共卫生建议包括避免共用垫子、使用前对表面进行消毒以及对不明原因的皮肤损伤寻求医疗评估。该案例是孤立的,没有证据表明存在更广泛的 传播 或系统性影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-08-20;所提供的证据中未明确报告事件日期。
地点与对象
中国武汉;患者,28岁,女性。
事件
武汉一名28岁女性因长期使用共用瑜伽垫被诊断患有低危皮肤HPV感染引起的扁平疣;该感染与与 宫颈癌 相关的 高危型HPV 类型无关,并且不具有致癌性。

单一确诊病例;没有死亡或系统性传播的报道。这种感染是良性的,与癌症风险无关。传播 是通过皮肤直接接触受污染的公共瑜伽垫而发生的。
应对、状态与不确定性
经武汉市第三医院皮肤科医生诊断;治疗 选项包括冷冻疗法或激光去除。公共卫生指南强调避免共用垫子,使用消毒湿巾,并就持续性皮肤病变寻求医疗咨询。没有表明正在进行的 暴发 调查或接触者追踪。该文章是一篇医学科普文章;没有描述正式的公共卫生应对措施。

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
女子感染的是哪种HPV类型,会致癌吗? - 新浪财经
News Brief
A 28-year-old woman in Wuhan, China, was diagnosed with flat warts caused by a low-risk cutaneous HPV infection after repeatedly using a shared yoga mat. Health authorities clarified that the strain involved is not linked to high-risk HPV types associated with cervical cancer and poses no oncogenic risk. The infection is benign and treatable with standard dermatological procedures such as cryotherapy or laser therapy. Public health advice includes avoiding shared mats, disinfecting surfaces before use, and seeking medical evaluation for unexplained skin lesions. The case is isolated, with no evidence of broader transmission or systemic impact.

女子感染的是哪种HPV类型,会致癌吗? 2026年08月21日 00:10 看点资讯 语音播报 缩小字体 放大字体 微博 微信 分享 0 腾讯QQ QQ空间 武汉一名28岁女子因长期使用公用瑜伽垫,后背冒出成片褐色小疙瘩,就医后确诊为皮肤型HPV(人乳头瘤病毒)感染引发的扁平疣——这是与宫颈癌无关的低危型HPV,不会致癌,无需过度恐慌。 一、感染的是哪类HPV:低危型皮肤HPV,非宫颈癌相关型别 病毒亚型:该女子感染的是引起扁平疣的低危型皮肤HPV,主要通过皮肤直接接触传播,并非通过性接触传播。 关键区别:HPV家族有200多种亚型,高危型(如HPV16、18等)持续感染才与宫颈癌相关,而本次案例属于皮肤低危型,与宫颈癌完全是两回事。 临床表现:感染后后背出现褐色或淡肤色扁平丘疹,呈片状分布,可伴瘙痒,抓挠后沿抓痕呈线性排列。 二、感染途径与致病条件:公用瑜伽垫是感染源头 病毒存活环境:公用瑜伽垫缝隙易残留汗液、皮屑,夏季湿热环境下HPV病毒可在垫面存活数小时。 入侵机制:运动时皮肤与垫面摩擦产生肉眼难见的微小破损,后背、手臂直接接触垫面,病毒"乘虚而入";出汗后皮肤角质层软化、屏障减弱,感染风险更高。 诊断依据:武汉市第三医院皮肤科主治医师刘睿检查后确诊为典型扁平疣,追问发现该女子练习瑜伽3个月一直使用公用垫、从未自备。 三、风险评估与科学应对:可治愈、可预防,不必恐慌 良性病变:低危型HPV引起的扁平疣属于良性皮肤增生,不具有癌变潜能,通过规范治疗(如冷冻、激光)可去除。 防护建议:运动时尽量自备瑜伽垫或铺隔脏毛巾;必须使用公用垫时,先用消毒湿巾擦拭并等垫面干透;皮肤有擦伤、裂口时避免直接接触公共垫子。 就医提示:身上出现不明原因、持续增多的小疙瘩,应及时到正规医院皮肤科就诊,切勿自行用药或抠抓,以免病毒扩散。 注:以上内容仅为医学科普,不构成诊疗建议,如有皮肤异常请及时线下就医。 0条评论|0人参与网友评论 表情 登录|注册 |退出 分享到微博 发布 最热评论 刷新 最新评论 刷新 更多精彩评论>> 相关新闻 加载中 点击加载更多 头条号入驻 财经自媒体联盟更多自媒体作者 今日推荐 优秀作者 看点月榜 盘点全球具身智能30强:谁能让机器人真正工作? View five news elements
Time
Published 2026-08-20; event date not explicitly reported in the supplied evidence.
Location and population
Wuhan, China; 28-year-old female patient.
Event
A 28-year-old woman in Wuhan was diagnosed with flat warts caused by a low-risk cutaneous HPV infection after prolonged use of a shared yoga mat; the infection is unrelated to high-risk HPV types linked to cervical cancer and is not oncogenic.
Scale, impact and risk
Single confirmed case; no deaths or systemic spread reported. The infection is benign and not associated with cancer risk. Transmission occurred via direct skin contact with a contaminated public yoga mat.
Response, status and uncertainty
Diagnosed by a dermatologist at Wuhan Third Hospital; treatment options include cryotherapy or laser removal. Public health guidance emphasizes avoiding shared mats, using disinfectant wipes, and seeking medical consultation for persistent skin lesions. No active outbreak investigation or contact tracing is indicated. The article is a medical science communication piece; no formal public health response is described.
